# Doctor lint precision ledger

One row per lint: how often its findings were real the last time anyone measured, on how many, and how many verified defects it caught. Asked for in #819: before this ledger, each batch's hand-check lived in an issue comment, and the next batch measured the same ratio again from scratch.

The rule is #819's: a hand-check that does not update this file did not happen. A lint PR re-measures its own row with the commands below and adds a measurement id. A hand-check appends rows to the history table at the end.

## How to read a row

- **hits**: the lint's findings over the scored runs (status `ran`). **warn+** is how many of those were WARN or ERROR.
- **judged TP / partial / FP**: the verdicts an adversarial verifier gave the lint's findings while auditing the batch. **TP / judged** is the hand-checked precision. Only the EBYSBC batch has verdicts.
- **matched / hits**: hits that name the same entry (`element_idx_start`) as a verified autopsy finding on the same run. This is a lower bound on precision: the autopsy recorded the defects it verified, not every true signal, so an unmatched hit still needs a hand-check before it counts as a false positive. It also counts a hit on the right entry for the wrong reason as a match. "names text" means the lint's findings quote text and name no entry, so they cannot be matched by index.
- **caught**: the verified findings this lint matched, which is its contribution to recall.
- **Evidence cap.** The doctor prints at most 3 evidence items per finding: the stage-6 re-emit in `doctor/lints/render.py` slices to 3, and `offschema_fields` keeps `FIELD_EVIDENCE_MAX_VALUES = 3` (`doctor/lints/extraction.py`). The scorer reads only the indices a finding prints. A finding about more entries is matched on the 3 it lists, so on the reroute rows and `offschema_fields`, matched and caught can undercount, and they depend on which 3 entries come first. On M1 the cap hides 85 of 206 reroute indices (in 11 of 72 findings) and 19 of 89 `offschema_fields` indices (in 5 of 47 findings). M1's doctor was re-run once with both caps lifted, in a scratch copy that is not committed: no matched, caught or recall number changed. Only the reroute rows' unmatched-index lists grew, `reroute_refused` from 57 to 92 indices and `reroute_same_family` from 16 to 46. Another arm can differ, so a lint PR on one of these rows says how many of its findings hit the cap.
- `stage6_render_warnings` is one lint key that re-emits about twenty unrelated stage-6 checks, so it has one row per message shape. The shapes are defined in `doctor/precision.py` (`_STAGE6_SHAPES`), which `scripts/doctor_vs_autopsy.py` imports.

## Who reads this file

`doctor/precision.py` parses the per-lint table below at run time, so keep its shape: the first table under the "Per-lint precision" heading, a `lint` column of backticked keys (a `stage6_render_warnings` shape row adds `: ` and the backticked shape), and a `TP / judged` column of `<tp> / <judged>` or `none`. Columns are found by header name. Shape rows are pooled into their lint key, because a finding carries the key. A lint listed twice keeps its first row. What it feeds:

- Each run's doctor report gets a `lint_precision` block, and the top lints in `doctor.tsv` and the lint on the Teams card's Doctor line carry `p~0.50 n=8` (TP / judged, and judged), or `p unmeasured`.
- The review copy's precision gate (#1589), `precision.shown_in_place`: a WARN or ERROR finding is a Word comment on the text it is about only when its row's TP / judged is at least 50%, or it has no verdicts. Below that it is left out of the copy (#2002) and listed only on the admin run page's Diagnostics tab. This reads rows unpooled: a `stage6_render_warnings` finding reads its shape's row (none for a shape with no row), and another lint's finding reads the row of the shape its message names, else its rows pooled. The rows are `precision.load_gate_ledger`'s: each per-lint row plus the held-out verdicts of the YUY-HO table below, for a lint whose code has not changed since the dev-259 doctor those verdicts judged (Paul, 2026-10-08). The changed lints and shapes are `precision.HELD_OUT_CHANGED`; a PR that changes another lint adds it there. A held-out row with no per-lint row brings the YUY-HO table's in-sample figures; a held-out row pooled over a lint the per-lint table splits by shape is not folded. Meant to be shared with the quality score (PR #1642) and the run page's fix list (PR #1640), open as of this change.
- #813's remediation gate, `precision.remediation_allowed`: a lint may drive a section retry only if it is one of #813's LLM-judgement lints (`missed_headers`, `segmentation`, `under_extraction`) with a TP / judged of at least 80% on at least 20 judged findings. On M1, none qualifies.

W3B-SC hand-checked every hit of its two lints, not only the unmatched ones. The first version of `section_consistency` had 29 hits, and a blind verifier found one false positive: MQSUIC 241, a bare bibliography URL coded S0, which `src/unified_pipeline/core/taxonomy_v7.json` lists as a typical S0 entry. That made it 28 of 29 (97%). The bare-URL branch of `cross_reference_as_record` now allows S0, which removes exactly that hit, so the shipped lint is 28 of 28 (every hit checked). The 14 unmatched `section_consistency` hits are 7 grant-review headings whose rows are coded Q2, 1 grant-review heading whose rows are coded I (SJWASY), 4 journal guideline or protocol articles coded S5 under a peer-reviewed heading or among peer-reviewed siblings (RGUNJV, VYICGW, ZGBCIT twice), and 2 BLS/ACLS/PALS lines coded F1/F2. The rules were written from these same 63 runs, so the precision is in-sample.

The RCBKFG autopsy (2026-10-05) added a `training_as_degree` shape to `section_consistency`: an intern or resident row coded B1 (#1415). Over the 96 local analysis runs with stored stage JSON (83 distinct source documents), rendered and doctored from origin/dev `8cafcd4c` with and without it, it adds one finding, on no other run, and changes no other finding: GKAQHB, entries 17 and 20, which that autopsy verified (its N1). RCB-HE re-ran the lint on origin/dev `6592d5eb` over RCB-D's 69 runs (each run's stored stage-3b JSON): `training_as_degree` fires once there too, on GKAQHB, and on none of the 62 labelled runs. That finding names two entries and counts as one hit, as RCB-D's JJUQDF finding (entries 342-346) does. The `section_consistency` row below counts it: 30 of 30 hand-checked. GKAQHB has no label file, so the hits, matched and caught columns, which describe the 62 labelled runs, do not change.

The NECRGC autopsy (2026-10-11) added an `attended_as_teacher` shape to `section_consistency` (#1691): a K4 or S8 entry that opens as attendance ("Attended", "Participated in", "Completed", "Attendee", "Participant"), or that sits under a leaf heading, or a sub-heading paragraph coded T, naming professional or career development, training, certifications or continuing education, with no teaching or authoring part (`stage6/parsing/text.py` `names_teaching_or_authorship_role`). It reads each run's stored stage-3b JSON, so no render is needed. Over the 22 NECRGC runs whose stage-3b JSON is on S3 it fires on 5 runs, 19 entries, every one an item the autopsy verified (HVSKET 8, LFFKVV 4, YMDTEE 3, XUTTKJ 2, MQLKPQ 2). It misses WBZIDP 62 and MQLKPQ 107, which sit under honors and affiliations headings, and MQLKPQ 29, which stage 6 rerouted from B2 (`stage6_render_warnings` reports that one). Over the 159 stage-3b artifacts of the farm and the 2026-09-11/-17 batches, the first version fired on 12 runs and 128 entries. Hand-reading every hit found 51 false: a grants list under a "Research and Training" parent (Opresko, 36), a heading that names teaching (web215 "National Teaching and Training", 7; web172 "Formal Teaching of Peers (e.g., CME and other continuing education courses)", 1), abstracts whose author list the first pattern missed (Ratner 5, Denckla 1), and a "Participant/Presenter" line (Cook 1). The first author pattern also hid one true hit, Opresko 70.1, whose "Southwestern PA," read as an author. The shipped version reads the leaf heading only, skips a heading that names a teaching role, counts "trainer" as a role, reads two or more surname-and-initials names or an APA "(2020). Title" as an author list, and does not read "Participant/Presenter" as attendance. It fires on 6 runs and 76 entries there, and all 76 read as attended (Opresko and web207 "Attendee"/"Participant" rows, web190's nursing continuing-education list, web199's equity trainings with their hours, YZTS9G's teaching workshops, web206's "Completion of" line). web185's two meetings stopped firing once "talks" in the sub-heading "Selected ... invited external talks:" counted as a teaching role; they were talks the owner gave. The PR #1829 review then narrowed that carve-out: a heading that names attendance ("CME Lectures Attended") is no carve-out, and "grand rounds" and a bare "invited" name a role in a heading only, never in entry text. The 76 entries did not change. The rules were tuned on these same runs, so the precision is in-sample.

The YUYVIG autopsy (2026-10-08) added two shapes to `section_consistency` (#1577). Both read each run's stored stage-3b JSON, so no render is needed. `honor_as_funding` (WARN): a grant code (M2, M2A, M2B or M2C) under a leaf heading that names honors, awards, prizes, recognition or distinctions and names no funding word (grant, fund, support, contract, research, investigator, current, past, pending, completed, ongoing, active), on a line with neither of the grant markers stage 3b's #1618 H pin uses (a grant or activity number, an amount, a two-ended period) nor a PI role at its head. `fellowship_as_membership` (INFO): an I entry that opens with an election to a fellowship ("Elected Fellow", "Elected to Fellowship", "Named Fellow", "Inducted as a Fellow"), or that names a fellowship as a title ("Fellow, <body>", "Fellow of <body>", "<Society> Fellow", with "Fellow" followed by a comma, of/in, a bracket or a digit; "<ACRONYM> Fellow" is not matched, since "NIH Fellow" or "AHA Fellow" is as often a training or funding fellowship) on a heading path naming honors whose leaf names no membership, society, organization, affiliation, certification or licensure. It is INFO because the stage-3b prompt (rules 16 and 27) codes a society fellowship I, and #1577's taxonomy decision is open. Over 335 distinct stage-3b artifacts (39 YUYVIG, 40 NECRGC/JAFPFP, 98 farm, 158 other local analysis runs), `honor_as_funding` fires 8 findings on 8 runs, 13 entries, and `fellowship_as_membership` 28 findings on 27 runs, 35 entries; every other `section_consistency` finding is unchanged (157 before, 193 after). On YUYVIG, `honor_as_funding` fires on exactly the autopsy's shape-1 entries (UVZNIC 117/118/120/121/122/126, ZDOAZO 333, IXJMKS 112) and on no other entry. Every one of its 13 entries was hand-read. 12 are award lines at model confidence 0.80 or below, the #1618 pin's own gate, on the runs whose recoded entries #1577's paid stage-4 A/B judged correct as honors (the 8 above, plus DXAGUS 51, HJPBEM 277, MUHLLD 70 and 2055 994); the 13th, 1BFKKK 24 (a seed award at 0.85 among dated grant awards under the same honors heading), is counted false: 12 of 13 (92%). It misses NECRGC's UAURFU, RBPXCD, AOJEQD and SPIHVQ honors-as-funding entries by design: each carries a K-series number or a two-ended period, the markers the pin keeps a grant code for. `fellowship_as_membership` finds the 3 YUYVIG targets (MVUREJ 44, IZIXVF 55, XNWSZN 53) and 6 of the 7 NECRGC ones (ZASOTV, XYXIQJ, YIXQFB, AOJEQD, LWMSPJ, UAURFU); it misses HBALTD 26, the autopsy's borderline one, under a Professional Certifications leaf. It also misses SQMWHM 1536 ("NATA Fellow, June 2012"), the one corpus hit of the dropped acronym form, and MVUREJ 760, the same ACMI fellowship as MVUREJ 44 listed again under a "Professional Organizations" leaf with no honors heading on its path, which is where the author put it. All 35 entries are a fellowship, coded I, under an honors heading or opening with an election, so all match #1577's shape; 14 open with an election and 21 are a "Fellow, <college or society>" title, the form prompt rule 16 names as I. Its precision is therefore 35 of 35 against #1577's shape and depends on that decision against the prompt. The rules were written from these runs, so both figures are in-sample.

RCB-D is the RCBKFG batch's doctor section (d). It changes seven lints and leaves every other lint's findings unchanged on all 69 runs: base and branch differ in 24 findings, each listed here and each hand-read against the stage-4 entry and the rendered docx (`w:ins` text included).

- `multi_record_coverage` (+3, all true): it now reads month/two-digit-year dates ("7/05") and apostrophe years ("'96"). CAOACN 32 and 64 (RCBKFG N1, KYOPUV-01/02: 4 appointments and 3 task forces lost) and YOXXOH 93 (verified YOXXOH-uid-4, a second training program lost). CAOACN 62 and 88 stay silent: 62 has `stage4_records` and four-digit years, and 88 is undated with no title word.
- `junk_or_header_row` (+7, all true): `role_only` (a duty sentence rendered as a row holding only the role stage 4 read: GKAQHB and MRJDWE 79, 84, verified MRJDWE-07), `description_only` (a description-only record whose sentence fills the committee row: GKAQHB 94), and a lead-in label rendered with its first word cut (JJUQDF and AQCLHS 326, verified AQCLHS-05). A first `role_only` draft, with `title` as a role field and no prose test, fired 55 times on D1, K1 and R rows that are records; the shipped rule fires only on the 4 above.
- `duplicate_records` (+3, all true): two bodies one letter apart (not a digit, 60 characters or more) are one record. UYFRTL and EQADVR (same source; RCBKFG N6, two abstracts the CV lists twice) and VVRTUC (one book chapter listed twice, same authors, book and pages, one title with a plural).
- `section_consistency` (+1, true): new shape `reviewer_journal_as_editorial_board`, JJUQDF 342-346 (RCBKFG NEW-01). On the farm, every other Q4C entry with no board or editor word sits under a heading that names a board, or names a role ("Member, ... Panel", BFSUMA 170, which stays quiet).
- `wrong_start_date` (-7): a grant renders "-Present" only where its source leaves the start year open, so the lint asks `format_date_range` with the entry's text, as the grant renderer does. FLYBMX and ZDCXIV 157, 174, 175 render one award date (RCBKFG FLYBMX: 0 of 3), and RGUNJV 314 now renders its source years with no "-Present", so the shape the verified RGUNJV-06 described is gone from the current render. The one recall match lost (RGUNJV-06, s7ab-13) is that finding.
- `enrichment_failures` (-1): a `title_check_failed` whose `enrichment_rejected.shared_pmid_with` is set is stage 5 putting the CV's own citation back; JJUQDF 93 is that correct rejection (RCBKFG, the fix for AQCLHS-02).
- `contact_slot_lost` (1 replaced): CAOACN 3 no longer names the home number stage 4 also filed as `home_phone`, and now names the office address filed under `work_address` beside the home `address`, which the rendered Office address row lacks (RCBKFG; appended to #1222).

The 62 labelled runs score as before except: `junk_or_header_row` matched 65 to 68, `multi_record_coverage` matched 84 to 85, `wrong_start_date` hits 9 to 5 (matched 8 to 5), `duplicate_records` unlocated 2 to 4, and recall 191 to 190 of 487 (the RGUNJV-06 match above). Every new shape was written from these runs, so the precision is in-sample; the next batch is the first out-of-sample check.

RCB-D2 is the out-of-sample check of RCB-D that its blind review asked for. On the 126 runs, RCB-D's code added 9 findings and raised 3 `duplicate_records` counts by 4 pairs. Each was hand-read against the stage-4 entry and the rendered docx (`w:ins` text included). 3 of the 13 were true:

- `multi_record_coverage` (+4, none true). Three WARNs read a two-digit date that is a note on the record as a second record: a grant's no-cost extension end written as a month and two-digit year (2068 537), a degree's conferral month written the same way (web187 16), and a slashed two-digit reference to a well-known date (web200 153). The fourth, an INFO on web199 776, split a mentee record at its month and two-digit year. Each of these entries also writes a four-digit year. RCB-D2 reads a two-digit date only in an entry that writes no four-digit year, which removes all 4. The RCB-D true positives (CAOACN 32 and 64, YOXXOH 93) write none.
- `junk_or_header_row` `role_only` (+4, 2 true). web200 150 (a duty sentence rendered as its role alone) and web204 277 (an award's name lost; the row shows only the role) are true. web40 150 and 154 are false: L2 projects whose name and `launch_date` render on the line above the role sentence. RCB-D2 counts the schema's other one-date fields (`launch_date`, `issue_date`, and so on) as dated, which removes those 2.
- `duplicate_records` (+4 pairs: web218, web227 twice, web244; none true). Each is one abstract or talk the CV lists at two meetings. The render prints authors, title and year only, so the two bodies end up one letter apart (a spelling, an author initial, a group name). RCB-D2 applies the one-letter rule only to a body that names a volume or page. The RCB-D pairs (UYFRTL, EQADVR, VVRTUC) all do.
- `section_consistency` (+1, true). `reviewer_journal_as_editorial_board` on web46: 8 journals coded Q4C under 'Personal Data', after an "Ad hoc Reviewer" line.

With RCB-D2, base (origin/dev `6592d5eb`) and branch differ on the 126 runs in exactly the 10 false or doubtful findings above: 6 findings fewer (2,213 to 2,207), and the 3 `duplicate_records` counts back to what they were before RCB-D, and on the 69 RCB-D runs in none (1,806 findings both). `contact_slot_lost` also skips an office-keyed address that repeats the home `address`, as stage 6 does since #1425; it moved no finding on either set. The per-lint rows below still count the 69 RCB-D runs only: the 126-run verdicts are on unlabelled runs and stay in this paragraph.

RCB-SC added `span_count` and changed no other lint's findings on any of the 245 runs (base code against branch code, both over the base render). It fired 118 times: 70 on the 119 `analysis` runs (61 top-level, 9 pilot) and 48 on the 126 farm and batch runs. Every hit was read against its entry's source text, and the lint itself checks that the record's rendered line shows the envelope. 111 are a row showing one range over years or terms the source lists separately with a gap: lists of reviewing or committee years, course offerings by term, and two records fused into one entry (NJIKGI 134, VYNARH 152, VQFSDI 147). 7 are counted false: KYOPUV 42 and 43, a certification and its recertification, which really are one continuous span; CYOFWJ 66, a membership held since its first year whose sub-roles carry the other years; OIYKZE 420, a range whose two ends a table cell split, with 'and' inside the course name between them; web36 449, one range written 'May, 2015 – November, 2019', whose month commas read as a list; and web227 738 and 743, two elected offices listed as '2018, 2021' and '2020, 2023', which may be terms rather than separate years (doubtful, counted false). 111 of 118 (94%): 66 of 70 on the `analysis` runs, 45 of 48 on the farm and batch runs. Some runs are the same CV run twice (UYFRTL and EQADVR, NTCULM and QFJSXR, GKAQHB, MRJDWE and RWBQKF), so their hits are counted once per run. Read by one reviewer, not an adversarial verifier. The parsing rules (months and term words, 'to' and 'between ... and', the list separator, the skip of mentoring codes) were written from the `analysis` runs, so 66 of 70 is in-sample; the farm and batch runs were not used to write them, and 45 of 48 there is the out-of-sample figure. Recall on the verified findings: UYFRTL 33, 34 and 48 and EQADVR 48 fire; UYFRTL 49 does not, because `_record_lines` finds no line for it. On the branch's own render (the stage-6 envelope fix in the same change) those 4 stop firing and 114 hits remain on 64 runs. 107 of them are real, on 61 runs, and every one of those records carries only `start_date` and `end_date`, with no further-span key, so stage 6 has nothing to show in place of the range. That residual is on #1245.

RC-ROLE: every hit was hand-read against the grant's own text. On the 106 `analysis/` runs, 3 hits on 2 runs: KUUKNJ 243 and 247 are verified finding KUUKNJ N8 (the owner listed under "PIs:" rendered co-I, and listed under "co-Is:" rendered PI). ZCTARO 247 is the same CV's earlier run, which the KUUKNJ report already names as wrong there; it is matched by reading, not by a verified finding of its own. Those shapes were written from those runs, so 3 of 3 is in-sample. The 126 farm/batch runs were out of sample, and the first version of the lint gave 14 hits there, 12 of them true: web188 33 (the owner's name followed by "(PI)", rendered co-I) and 11 web30 grants (the owner as "Name of PD/PI", or as "(MPI)" beside another PI, all rendered co-I). That is 12 of 14 (86%) out of sample and 15 of 17 (88%) overall. The 2 false positives were label shapes the first version misread: web26 705 "Co PI: <owner>" (a co-PI written with a space, read as "PI:") and web241 150 "(PI: <other>, Subcontract PI: <owner>)" over "Role: Co-Investigator" (a subaward's PI, read as the grant's PI). Both labels now name no role, which leaves 15 hits, all true. That 15 of 15 counts the two fixed shapes, so it is not out of sample. Of the 280 grants on the first 97 `analysis/` runs whose text gives the owner exactly one role, 197 agree with `pi_role`, 3 contradict it, and the rest carry an empty or other role, which the lint does not judge. CAOACN N4 (owner named PI, `pi_role` empty) is that unjudged case: an empty role lands on 58 grants across 13 of those runs, 53 of them with the owner in `pi_name`, so it is not reported.

RC-ROLE2 adds the four grant-table shapes the EOAHMI recheck found and no lint flagged (#1410 side effects). Each finding names its shape in the message. Base and branch differ only in `role_consistency` findings, on every run and arm: the 15 RC-ROLE findings come back with the shape name in their message, and the new findings are the hits below; no other lint's findings changed (stored docx 4,202 to 4,361 findings; `analysis/` render 3,241 to 3,259; farm render 2,267 to 2,284). The stage-6 change in the same PR only names the grant table's row labels as constants, and a render of the 126 farm runs from the branch matches the base render (`render_gate_compare.py`: paragraph CHANGED 0, fingerprint CHANGED 0). Every hit was read against its entry's source text, except the `pi_cell_empty` hits that carry a PI label, which were checked by script (no PI label in any of them names a person other than the owner) and sampled by hand (26 read).

- `pi_cell_empty` (WARN): a rendered grant table whose "Your role:" says PI and whose "Name of Principal Investigator:" is empty. It reads the render, because #1446 now fills that cell with the owner on a bare "PI" role. On the stored documents it fires 143 times on 24 of the 119 `analysis/` runs, including all 11 JIJRSN trials of JIJRSN-01; on the current render it fires twice there (TVZDVF 71 and 76, "<owner>/<other>, MPI", whose MPIs render only as co-investigators) and 16 times on 4 farm runs ("MPI" or "Contact PI" roles, which #1446 deliberately leaves unfilled). 157 of 159 are true. The 2 partials are a real defect other than the one named: NDXXAD 638 (stage 4 invented the PI role, the cviche-d0 watch item) and web205 841 (the source says Co-PI; the role renders Contact PI). 99%, n=159: WARN.
- `pi_also_co_i` (INFO): `pi_name` is one of the people `co_investigators` lists, and the table renders both rows. 7 hits, all true: KDAZOM 363, 364, 365 and 380 (the PI repeated under Co-Investigators, with "Dr." or ", PI") and MQSUIC 153, 161 and 163 (a student co-PI rendered as the PI of the owner's faculty-PI award). The first version also fired on 30 web204 grants whose co-investigator list is the source's author line, headed by the PI and naming the owner; the PI cell there is right, so those count as partial, and the shape now skips that list (in-sample). n=7: INFO.
- `pi_from_collaborator` (INFO): `pi_name` is a person the text names only after "with" ("with Dr. X"), where no role word labels anyone and no role is stated for the owner. 5 hits, all true, all JIJRSN: 150, 152, 154 and 156 (JIJRSN-02) and 148, the same shape in a record the recheck did not list (its twin VYNARH 148 already named the collaborator as PI). n=5: INFO.
- `owner_lead_as_co_i` (INFO): an unlabelled grant with no `pi_name` and no stated role, whose `co_investigators` list and text both name the owner first. 5 hits, all true: QTATUP 529, 533 and 537 (QTATUP-01), BMHBJZ 644 and LDPDKA 91, both "<owner>, <other>" lines with no label. The first version also fired on NDXXAD 411, where the owner's stated role, a program title that is not a lead role, is what renders; the shape now needs no stated role. n=5: INFO.

Recall on the EOAHMI recheck's #1410 regressions in grant tables (JIJRSN-01, JIJRSN-02, QTATUP-01, DUTAVD-04), read against each run's stored stage-4 JSON and docx: JIJRSN-01, 11 of 11 records (`pi_cell_empty`); JIJRSN-02, 4 of 5 records (150, 152, 154, 156 by `pi_from_collaborator`; 156 would also be `pi_also_co_i`, which the entry reports once); QTATUP-01, the 3 of 9 grants where the owner is listed first (`owner_lead_as_co_i`). Not caught: JIJRSN 562 (the owner is the PI of a trial another investigator initiated, and stage 4 made the initiator `pi_name`), QTATUP's 6 grants led by someone else (an empty PI cell on a grant the owner did not lead names no owner role to check), and DUTAVD-04 (a plural "Investigators" label left `pi_role` empty, which no shape judges). LOOTTE-01 is a mentee field, not a grant. On the dev-248 documents the EOAHMI doctor caught 0 of these; this catches 3 of the 4 grant findings, at 18 of their 26 records. Every shape was written from these runs, so this precision is in-sample.

X6-role adds the five grant-table shapes of the X6 batch's class E32 (wrong-field values on grants) that the dev-246 doctor missed. Base and branch differ only in these findings: no `role_consistency` finding was removed or changed on any of the 359 runs, and 483 were added. `scripts/doctor_gate.py` over the whole doctor, base and branch, on the farm arms (X6 live documents and dev render, NDMRSO live documents and fresh render, EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot, EOAHMI, and the 126 runs) adds only these `role_consistency` findings and removes none: every other lint's findings are identical on every run (X6 live 191 to 212, X6 dev 178 to 193, NDMRSO live 775 to 809, NDMRSO fresh 728 to 756, EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 1,671 to 1,759, EOAHMI 102 to 103, the 66 output runs 999 to 1,015, batch-3 850 to 927, batch-4 465 to 496). The table below counts every run, so one CV stored as several runs (the X6 and NDMRSO runs appear under `analysis/` and on their farm render; KJJVVO, FLYBMX, UPFAYD and ZDCXIV are one source document) counts once per run; the verdicts are per distinct record. Every hit of four shapes was read against its entry's source text and the rendered table (`w:t` text, `w:ins` included, through the doctor's table reader); `owner_pi_role_empty` was checked by script and sampled by hand.

- `owner_also_co_i` (WARN): the owner is listed under Co-Investigators while the stated role is a lead role (a PI of any kind, a director, a mentor), or no role is stated and the owner's own co-investigator item names one. 117 hits, 78 distinct records on 25 CVs. 77 true: a Co-PI, MPI, site PI, mentor or director shown again among the co-investigators (IEUPKK 257-342, verified IEUPKK-23 on the X6 runs; BIITRL 269, verified BIITRL-07; web32's seven site-PI grants; TVZDVF 59, 71, 76, 159; and others), and the owner's role left in the co-investigator cell with "Your role:" empty (RVTAQT 223, verified RVTAQT-09; WIANVH 590). 1 partial: web26 709, where stage 4 put a program's subproject PIs, the owner among them, into `co_investigators` beside a correct Program Director role. A first draft fired on any stated role other than co-I; it is now limited to lead roles and spares a role that also names a co-investigator or an investigator (IZJADE 433), a "PI: <name>" label in the role field, and NDXXAD 411's non-lead program title. 77 / 78 (99%), in-sample.
- `owner_pi_role_empty` (WARN): the rendered PI cell names the owner and "Your role:" is empty (RVTAQT 260 and 273, verified RVTAQT-09). This is the empty-`pi_role` case RC-ROLE left unreported. 275 hits, 193 distinct records on 54 CVs. Every one was checked by script: the PI cell's matching person is the owner (the surname alone, with the owner's initial, or with another given name the owner goes by; none is a different person sharing the surname), and the role cell is empty. 44 were read by hand, one per CV where the CV has a placed record: all true. Of the 193, the source labels the owner PI in 91, gives the owner's line no role label in 66 (unlabelled author lines whose first name stage 4 read as the PI), and labels the owner with another lead role in 25 (co-PIs, which still leave the role empty); 11 tables match no stage-4 title and are reported without an index. The shape reads only the render, so it does not judge whether the owner really led the grant, only that the table leaves the owner's role blank beside the owner's name. 193 / 193 (100%). Most of it sits on runs whose stage 4 predates #1410 (which writes `pi_role` "PI" when the label names the owner), so on new runs it guards that rule.
- `owner_pi_other_role` (WARN): the rendered PI cell names only the owner beside a role that makes the owner a co-, site- or sub-PI or a co-I, and no kind of PI besides. 82 hits, 66 distinct records on 26 CVs. 65 true: the stage-6 owner auto-fill beside a Co-PI or site-PI role that #1457 stopped (KJJVVO 168, 173, 176, 178 on the live document, verified KJJVVO-05; the same records on FLYBMX, UPFAYD and ZDCXIV; ATUVAL 541, verified ATUVAL-10; BRUSUZ 325, verified BRUSUZ-06; JIJRSN 540 and 656, verified JIJRSN-03; RNKYST's five site-PI trials; EOSAFF 308-320; XWNZWW 248-263; and others), a co-PI pair rendered as the owner alone (QZWBKQ 947, 988, 1000; RGUNJV 122, 163; TVZDVF 118, 179, 194; web244 748), and a stage-4 role that contradicts the source's PI slot (web244 673; web30's four "(PI name)" grants rendered Co-Investigator). 1 false: QZWBKQ 893, whose source names the owner both Co-PI and P.I. A first draft fired on every role `_role_names_owner_as_pi` rejects; 19 of its records were PI-equivalent roles (PD/PI, Program Director (PI), MPI, PD, a "Principle Investigator" typo), so the shape now names only co-, site- and sub-PI and co-I roles and spares any role that also holds a PI-equivalent. 65 / 66 (98%), in-sample. On the fresh `05966dac` render of the NDMRSO runs it fires 3 times, all TVZDVF (118, 179, 194: a stage-4 `pi_name` of the owner beside a Co-PI role), against 9 on those runs' stored documents, where the auto-fill had not yet been stopped.
- `role_in_title` (INFO): no role is stated and `title` holds a role as a list item (", Director ...", "; Mentor ..."). 5 hits, one record (RINASX 405 and its earlier runs DTFNOR and EQADVR), true (verified RINASX-09). The first draft also read a bare ", PI" item, which fired on XELRLZ 138, a title naming a pilot project's PI; that item is no longer read. n=3 distinct: INFO.
- `pi_cell_from_title` (INFO): the rendered PI cell is a run of two or more of the title's own words that stage 4 did not extract as `pi_name` and that does not name the owner. 4 hits, one record on four runs of one source (RINASX 396, verified RINASX-08; DTFNOR, EQADVR, UYFRTL), all on stored documents rendered before #1418. The first draft also fired on NDMRSO CAGLNY's six student fellowships titled after their holder, whose PI cell is stage 4's `pi_name`; the shape now spares a PI cell equal to it. n=4: INFO. Like `owner_pi_other_role`, it is a regression guard on current dev.

Blind verification of X6-role (2026-10-07) widened the corpus to 378 runs (the 19 `analysis/<uid>/s3` runs the 359 left out) and hand-read 11 hits on them: 8 true, 1 ambiguous (YME2VA 222, whose source contradicts itself) and 2 false, both `owner_pi_other_role` on a PI cell that joins two co-PIs with a slash (BYFQBG 322; YME2VA 236, reported as entry 176 by the first-entry-per-title placement). The person split did not cut on "/", so the pair read as one person who is the owner. It now does. Base `50c3058d` against the fix, `lint_role_consistency` over 475 runs (the 359 above plus the 19 `s3` and 97 `_s3` stored runs under `analysis/`): exactly those 2 findings removed, none added or changed. The split is shared with `co_investigators`, so a slash there now cuts too: two slash-joined people are two people (a PI among them is seen by `pi_also_co_i`), and an owner item "<owner> (Co-I/PI)" keeps only the role before the slash, so with no stated role `owner_also_co_i` no longer fires on it, as it does not on a stated "Co-I/PI" role; "<owner> (PI/Co-I)" still fires. No co-investigator value in the 475 runs changed a finding. One caveat on the stage-4 shapes' new hits: some stored documents predate #982's Co-Investigators row (BYFQBG 334, YME2VA 260), so on those documents the owner listed again among the co-investigators is not visible; a run rendered on current dev shows it.

| ROLE-REC | 2026-10-08 | origin/dev `2cae24d7` against the #1590 table placement, `lint_role_consistency` and `read_docx_table_rows` from both trees over the same stage-4 JSON and docx | X6-role's 359 runs and RC-ROLE2's 245, each rebuilt: the `analysis/` runs over their stored docx (RC-ROLE2: the 119 present on 2026-10-05), the X6 farm render, and fresh `scripts/render_gate.py` renders, of `05966dac` for X6-role's other farms and of `43f84e1e` for RC-ROLE2's `analysis/` render and 126 runs | as X6-role and RC-ROLE2 | #1590 |

ROLE-REC re-measures the render shapes per record. They placed every table at the first grant entry with its title, so grants sharing a title (an organisation's name as the title of each of its grants) were reported as that first entry, once per table, with identical message and evidence (YUYVIG SQMWHM: 1325 three times, 1331 and 1364 twice), and the hit counts above counted tables. Each table now takes the first same-title entry it has not matched, preferring one whose stated role is its "Your role:" cell, and tables that still resolve to one entry and shape are one finding with a count. The rebuilt corpora reproduce both rows on the base: on X6-role's 359 runs every run's `role_consistency` findings equal X6-role's recorded output, and on RC-ROLE2's runs `pi_cell_empty` fires 159 times on the stored documents and the 126-run render (143 and 16; the row left out the 2 TVZDVF hits on the `analysis/` render, and so does this). Base against branch, per shape:

- `pi_cell_empty`: 159 to 158 hits on RC-ROLE2's arms (164 to 163 on X6-role's). The one fewer is KDAZOM's two tables whose title no entry carries, now one "a grant table" finding counting 2. Distinct (run, entry) pairs 142 to 158: MQSUIC's 11 P.I. tables, MIFYLG's, BMAMWE's, JIJRSN 558/560 and VQFSDI 249/267 now name their own entries, each a stated PI role. Matched 11 to 10: MQSUIC 116 (MQSUIC-09) had two tables, and the second is now its own entry. TP / judged 156 / 158: the verdicts read the table, which placement does not change, and the KDAZOM pair counts once.
- `owner_pi_role_empty`: 275 to 277 hits, distinct records 193 to 198, CVs 54. The two new hits are web30 1115 and 1236, whose empty-role tables naming the owner the base placed on same-title entries stating Co-Investigator with an entry finding of their own, which suppressed them; both PI cells name the owner (checked as the 193 were). Moved to an entry with an empty stated role: QZWBKQ 847 to 1101 and 855 to 863, RVROVQ 85/101 to 93/111, WWSEWY 166 to 181, web228 190 to 497, web202 474 to 551, web26 246 to 746 (the base entries of WWSEWY, web228, web26 and QZWBKQ 847 state PI, Principal Investigator or Co-PI, so the base placement was wrong). Matched 24 to 26: RVROVQ 111 is in RVROVQ-02 (a grant split at stage 2, not a role defect, so caught does not change), on two runs; WWSEWY-05 stays matched at 181.
- `owner_pi_other_role`: 82 hits, unchanged; distinct records 66 to 70, the four runs of KJJVVO's source now reporting 177 apart from 176 (KJJVVO-05 verified 177 true), so TP / judged 69 / 70. Matched unchanged at 15 (ZDCXIV 176's second table moves to 177, which ZDCXIV-01 also names).
- `pi_cell_from_title` and every stage-4 shape: no finding changed.

After the fix every hit of these shapes is a distinct (run, entry) record on both corpora (X6-role: 163, 277 and 82 hits, 163, 277 and 82 records), and no two findings on a run share message and evidence (base: 24 such pairs on X6-role's runs, 26 on RC-ROLE2's). The matched counts are the base's published counts plus the index deltas above; a local scorer over `analysis/<uid>/*_verified.json` and the EBYSBC labels moves by the same amounts but does not reproduce every published total, since X6-role matched against a wider label set. The `pi_cell_empty`, `owner_pi_role_empty` and `owner_pi_other_role` bullets above keep their table counts as measured; the rows count records. The first A/B of the change, over the 184 stored runs with a stage-4 JSON and docx under `analysis/` (146 in the main checkout, 38 YUYVIG), gave 353 to 352 findings and 16 to 0 duplicate pairs.

Recall on the X6 batch's E32 findings, by record, over the live documents (`analysis/<uid>/stage_6_wcm_documents`) and the dev render (`render-x6-dev`): RVTAQT-09 3 of 3 on both (223 `owner_also_co_i`, 260 and 273 `owner_pi_role_empty`); IEUPKK-02 2 of 2 on both (243 and 247, `contradicted`, already on the base); IEUPKK-23 9 of 10 on both (257, 265, 269, 280, 284, 292, 296, 300, 342 `owner_also_co_i`; 261, where the source's second PI renders as a co-investigator, is not caught); KJJVVO-05 5 of 5 on the live document (`owner_pi_other_role`; 177 shares 176's title, so its table was reported as entry 176 until ROLE-REC's placement fix), 0 on the dev render, where #1457 fixed it; RINASX-08 4 of 4 on the live document (396 `pi_cell_from_title`, 382 and 388 `pi_cell_empty`, 393 the unplaced `pi_cell_empty` table, the last three already on the base), 0 on the dev render, where #1418 and #1446 fixed it; RINASX-09 1 of 1 on both (`role_in_title`). Not caught, and not grant-role shapes: RVTAQT-12 (an appointment's unit left in its title), IEUPKK-22 (a mentee's degree field as `research_focus`), KJJVVO-13 (a committee row printing the raw source line) and RINASX-12 (a mentee's externship as `funding_source`). Of the X6 batch's 10 E32 findings, 6 are grant findings (25 records). On the live documents the base caught 2 of them (IEUPKK-02, and RINASX-08 at 3 of its 4 records: 5 of 25 records); the branch catches all 6, at 24 of the 25 records. Outside X6, matched by index against every run's verified findings, the new shapes name five verified findings of their own shape that the base doctor missed: ATUVAL-10, BRUSUZ-06 and JIJRSN-03 (`owner_pi_other_role`), KHXOUF-14 (`owner_pi_role_empty`) and EQADVR-06 (`pi_cell_from_title`). The other index matches in the table (CTWLTR-01, CXRYCF-01, DXAGUS-02, VYRDHN-09, WWSEWY-05 and -06, BIITRL-07, IEUPKK-01, ATUVAL-11, KJJVVO-14, RXYBVF-02, ZDCXIV-01) are other defects on the same entries, so the caught column counts only the same-shape findings.

Owner matching. The shapes find the owner by `cv_owner.last_name`'s words, as the other owner lints do, and stage 4's surname is not ground truth for compound names. Measured over the 178 distinct CVs here (by owner name): 146 have grant entries, and 4 of those have no surname word long enough to judge. Of the other 142, the surname is in at least one grant's text on 80 and in none on 62 (CVs whose grants state only a role, such as "Role: PI"); 4 have a compound or hyphenated surname, whose parts each match. One CV names its owner in its grants by a name stage 4 did not file as the surname: web218, whose citation surname sits in `middle_name`. Adding `middle_name`'s words to the owner set changed no finding on any of the 359 runs, so it is not done. No owner word is a particle (one surname is a word that is a particle elsewhere, and is the owner's real surname there). Every PI cell the two owner render shapes read as the owner is the owner (above).

None of these shapes feeds the quality score: like `span_count` and the rest of `role_consistency`, the lint is not a `quality_score.py` input, and this change does not make it one. `owner_also_co_i`, `owner_pi_role_empty` and `owner_pi_other_role` clear the #822 bar (80% on 20 or more), but the defects they name are low-severity misstatements rather than lost content, so a content-loss cap would be the wrong use; that is a decision for #822, not this change.

14 of 20 true and 6 partial: 70% strict, 100% counting the partials. 12 of the 20 are the records the shape was written from (#1688 built it from #1571's lines), so in-sample; BCTOGR's 8 are the out-of-sample part, 2 true and 6 partial.

SC-1 adds `split_child_unsourced`: a record stage 4 split out of an entry (`stage4_records`, #1406) that shows a place or a date range from outside its own text. Each finding names its shape. Base and branch differ only in this lint's findings, on every run and arm: EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 1,830 findings both, NDMRSO 795 both, EOAHMI 125 to 126, EOAHMI on dev-248 138 to 141. Every hit was read against the stage-4 entry and the rendered row (read through `read_docx_table_rows`).

- `institution_from_outside_entry` (INFO): a record with no institution or organization, whose siblings name one, renders in a row with a place cell holding two or more words the entry's text does not. 1 hit, true: DUTAVD 30 on dev-248 (DUTAVD-03, the head-and-neck residency shown with the internship's hospital). It does not fire on the fresh render: #1449 stopped the training institution recovery there, so on current dev it is a regression guard. The first draft, at one foreign word, also fired on DUTAVD 88 on both arms, whose one-word cell is the "Member" role stage 6 supplies; two words removes it. Records whose siblings name no place either are skipped, because there the place comes from an employer or society heading above the entry: without that rule GHCIXA 70 (the society line above it) and ATUVAL 57 (the employer line above it, 4 records) fire, both correct renders, and so does DUTAVD 42, whose bare "Member" record matches another entry's "Member" row of the same year. n=1: INFO.
- `date_from_sibling` (INFO): two or more records carry one range that the entry writes fewer times than that, beside a record whose own range starts outside it, and, with the docx read, two of them render it. 3 hits, all true: WYMVGU 975 on both arms (four offices each shown with one shared open span; the source dates the board seat and the last office only, WYMVGU-02, still open on #1445) and WYMVGU 865 on dev-248 (an undated Past-President shown with the board term, the other half of WYMVGU-02). On the fresh render 865 renders no date (#1449's `_unsourced_date_records`), so the render check keeps it quiet there; read against stage 4 alone it would fire. Before the render check and the prose and inside-the-range rules, the shared-range test fired on 5 entries of the 216 split entries on the three farms: the 2 above, JBUVYV 346 (three years over four talks, every record sharing; no record of its own), JBUVYV 382 (the odd record's start year is the shared range's end) and CAGLNY 13 (a sentence giving three grants one year). Those three are the #1458 trap shape, one range given to every record, and the shipped rule spares all three (CAGLNY 13 by the prose test, which only matters without the docx: its grant tables have no row the render check finds). The trap the rule cannot separate is "2015 talk A, talk B, 2016 talk C": one year printed over two talks, which #1449 treats as sourced. No split entry on these farms or on the 56 `analysis/` runs whose stage 4 carries `stage4_records` has that layout. n=3: INFO.

Recall on the two EOAHMI recheck findings this lint is for: DUTAVD-03 (entry 30) and WYMVGU-02 (975 by index; 865 is in its description), both on dev-248, where no other lint names those entries. Matched by index: DUTAVD 30 and WYMVGU 975; 865 is caught but not matched. Both shapes were written from these runs, so the precision is in-sample, and n is 4. Neither shape changes the quality score: like `span_count` and `role_consistency`, the lint is not a `quality_score.py` input.

JUNK-EV changes only which row `junk_or_header_row` quotes as evidence: of the rows that match an entry, it quotes the first whose year agrees with the entry's (a year in its text or a date field), else the first. A finding is still emitted exactly when some row matches. Base and branch were run over the same renders, with a second base run as a determinism control (0 runs differ on every corpus). On all 294 runs no finding was added or removed, and no severity, message, other lint or metric changed (findings: 3,394 on the 105 `analysis` runs, 2,146 on the farm, 2,267 on the 126 runs; `junk_or_header_row` 198, 108 and 36 of them). 16 evidence lists changed, each read by hand, and each moved from a dated record to the entry's own row: QTATUP 1252, 1253 and 1261 and KHXOUF 1252 and 1261 (a society or PRN header's own `<organization> | Member` row, not the dated membership or office row before it), BNYLDF 620, GKAQHB 116, RWBQKF 116, IZJADE 491 and WYMVGU 491 (the same shape), BZZNRL 231 and 274 (an undated K4 label's own row, on both the `analysis` run and the farm) and CXRYCF 110 (an undated division header's own row, on both). None changed on the 126 runs. Verdicts that move: the EOAHMI recheck read QTATUP's 13 hits as 10 true and 3 (1252, 1253, 1261) real junk rows quoting a valid neighbouring row, so 13 of 13 now quote the row they flag; NDMRSO's KHXOUF review counted 1252 and 1261 short for quoting the valid membership rows, though the rows they name are real junk (KHXOUF's verified Q2 1252/1253/1261 finding), so KHXOUF is 14 of 15, the 15th (1286) an entry stage 6 dropped. UYQRUN 157 and 176 do not move: those entries render no row at all, and the row each quotes is another record's, which is a different defect (the lint should not fire), not a choice between two matching rows. Counts are unchanged, so `junk_or_header_rows` in `quality_score.py` (the #1443 cap input) is unchanged on every run.

FAN-RES adds `fanout_cell_residue`: a record stage 6 fanned out of a multi-record entry (`stage4_records`, #1406) whose grid-table row prints the parent's leftover text in a name, organization or committee cell. Five shapes: another record's year (`sibling_year`), a two-digit year tail such as '93' (`year_stub`), a range word 'to' opening or ending the cell (`bare_to`), a built record line '<role> | 1981 | 1981' (`built_line`), and another record's value with more text around it (`sibling_value`). A row belongs to the record whose values (and, to break a tie, years) its cells match best, and a cell is read only when every word of it is in the parent's text. Base and branch differ on every arm in exactly the new lint's findings: no other lint's finding, severity or metric moved (dev renders: EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 1,830 findings both, EOAHMI 125 to 126, NDMRSO 795 both; dev-248 EOAHMI 138 to 149; the `6d9862f0` re-render, EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 1,930 both and NDMRSO 833 to 834). On a run that fires, the report's `counts` and `lint_precision` blocks change with the finding; nothing else in it does. Every hit was read against its stage-4 entry and the rendered row (`w:ins` text included, through the doctor's table reader):

- dev-248, EOAHMI (recall): 11 findings on 3 runs, each on an entry of a verified finding: DUTAVD 82 and 79 (DUTAVD-01: the third term's organization cell holds the other two terms' years; 79's committee cells hold the role with every term's years, and a built line of the role and its year twice), WYMVGU 931-935 (WYMVGU-01: each second role's organization cell holds the first role and the year tail), and BRUSUZ 196, 228, 249 and 265 (BRUSUZ-01: the second talk's title cell holds the parent's whole line, the first talk's venue included). 3 of the 3 verified fan-out residue findings, 11 of their 11 entries. GHCIXA-01 (entry 70) is not caught and is not this shape: dedup dropped the record holding the parent's text, and the orphan sibling's row prints no leftover text, only a duplicate of entry 154's role and years with its organization blank. Catching it needs a cross-entry duplicate rule, which `duplicate_records` or `dedup_drops` would own.
- dev `8b287ec2`, 102 runs: 1 finding, BRUSUZ 196, true. #1449 keeps the parent's whole line as the last record's fallback when the records' fields leave words of it unheld (`fan_out.OWN_TEXT_KEY` is None), and stage 4 left this entry's venue names out of both records, so the second talk's row still prints the first talk's venue and an empty '( )'. #1449 fixed the other 10 entries above; this is the residual it names.
- `6d9862f0` re-render of the other 93 runs (EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 63, NDMRSO 30), out of sample: 1 finding, NDMRSO UYQRUN 117, true: two year-keyed offices, and the second's organization cell holds the first office and its year. The same run on the dev render has no finding: #1449 fixed it.

13 of 13 hand-checked (100%). The 11 dev-248 EOAHMI findings are in-sample (the rules were tuned on them: a first draft that tied a row to any record sharing one value flagged 102 rows on that render, mostly on rows of a sibling that shares the organization; requiring the row's best match, and treating a cell that IS another record's value as no residue, removed every one). The UYQRUN finding is the out-of-sample check, 1 of 1. A wider probe over the three dev-248 arms (any non-date cell made of a split entry's text that holds two records' years or two records' distinct values) found no fan-out residue the lint misses; its other hits were rows of records that share words, and one pre-existing date stub ('4/-12/') in a row of an entry stage 4 did not split, which is not this lint's shape. Own years in a record's own name cell are not read: on EBYSBC MQSUIC 611 the committee table prints every entry's whole line there, split or not. The `table_rows` view reads top-level tables only; every grid table with a date column in these 204 documents is top-level. The lint does not feed the quality score (`quality_score.py` imports only the lints it caps on), and none of the same kind (`span_count`, `role_consistency`) does either.

| IDR | 2026-10-05 | origin/dev `8b287ec2` plus `identical_rendered_rows`, base and branch over the same renders (`scripts/doctor_gate.py` with `--source-dir`) | 111: the 102 fresh renders of `8b287ec2` (no `--source-dir` at render; EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot 63, EOAHMI 9, NDMRSO 30), and the 9 EOAHMI runs over the dev-248 render (`6d9862f0`, before #1449), all over each farm's stored stage outputs | the EOAHMI recheck (QTATUP-04, BRUSUZ-01, DUTAVD-01); as M1 on the 62 labelled farm runs | EOAHMI QTATUP-04, BRUSUZ-01 |

IDR adds `identical_rendered_rows`: rows of one rendered table that a reader cannot tell apart. Neither existing duplicate lint is its home. `duplicate_records`' block rule compares enumerated paragraphs, and the `blocks` view holds a whole table as one block, so it cannot see a table row; its record rule pairs S1-S3 citations and grants by title, and each grant renders as a table of its own. Its claim is also the wrong one for QTATUP-04, which is seven real talks, not one record listed twice. `dedup_drops` reads stage 6's dedup decisions, which are rows removed, not rows kept. The lint reads the docx's table rows (`w:ins` text included; none of the 111 renders holds a nested table) and stage 4. A row is matched to the stage-4 records (each `stage4_records` child, or the entry) whose words hold all of the row's words, less a "Present" that stage 6 writes for an open end. It fires in two shapes, WARN, no score cap (as `duplicate_records`, which `quality_score.py` does not read either):

- `distinct_records`: two or more identical rows in one table whose matched records fill one field with different values. Identical rows whose records agree are silent: the CV lists that record twice. A field only one record fills is no disagreement.
- `fused_repeat`: a multi-record entry whose whole text renders as one row while a row holding one of its records, and no word the fused row lacks, renders in the same table.

The survey before the rule (every group of identical rows in one table, on all 111 renders) found 34 groups; each was read against the stage-4 entries and the rendered table. The first rule had a third shape, fewer matched records than identical rows: its 3 hits (SEKQUI 250, WYMVGU 981, IZJADE 981, the last two one CV) were all a membership the CV lists twice, one copy with an open year to which stage 6 adds "Present", so only one copy matched. That shape is dropped and "Present" is ignored, which also leaves those groups silent under `distinct_records`, where the two copies agree. Base and branch differ in exactly the lint's findings: 21 more (2,587 to 2,608); no other lint's findings and no run's worst severity changed on any of the 111 runs. Every hit was read against its stage-4 entries and the rendered docx:

- 15 on the 102 dev renders, all true: 14 groups of rows that hide the dates which tell them apart, because the template's date column shows the year only (CXRYCF 557/558; QNZADH 428/429/432, 459/463/465, 466/467, 468/472; VGHNZD 653/654, 656/657, 658/660, 852/853; ATUVAL 450/454; GCFEBE 286/287/288, 295/296; MQJAVH 464/468: one review panel or one talk on several dates in a year), and WIANVH 103/106, a board's two exam parts, whose part and year both drop, so both render as the board's name alone. KYOPUV 450/451 is the date shape too, and its rows also lose the two session titles (verified KYOPUV-07, the one matched hit). VGHNZD 852's third record (854) renders the venue in lowercase, so its row differs by case and is not counted among the identical ones.
- 6 on the dev-248 render, all true: QTATUP 980 (QTATUP-04, 7 rows), BRUSUZ 228, 249 and 265 (`fused_repeat`, BRUSUZ-01), DUTAVD 79 (`fused_repeat`: a term rendered as its own row and again inside a row holding the entry's whole text, which DUTAVD-01 describes but indexes as 82), and JBUVYV 822, one source line giving two dates in a year split into two records, the QTATUP-04 shape the recheck did not list.

21 of 21 (100%). The rule was written from these same runs, so the precision is in-sample. Recall on the dev-248 render: QTATUP-04 caught; BRUSUZ-01 3 of its 4 entries; DUTAVD-01 1 of its 2 (79; at 82 the leftover cell lost the role, so no row holds the whole text). BRUSUZ 196 is not caught: its first record, a city alone, rendered as a bare row that stage 6 dedup then dropped, so the first record shows only once, inside the fused row. On the current render neither defect shows (QTATUP 980 renders once, and #1449 fixed the fan-out residue at BRUSUZ 228, 249 and 265 and DUTAVD 79; BRUSUZ 196 still renders fused), so on new runs the lint guards against their return. Not caught, and left to other lints: RGUNJV 2889/2890 and ATUVAL 598/600, one source record split across two stage-2 entries that each carry the whole record (the records agree, so it reads as the CV listing it twice); VGHNZD 730/731, two projects whose names stage 4 left in the text only; and XELRLZ 23, an off-schema `titles` list whose second and third appointments render with the first one's department.

X6-grant adds a shape to `grant_boundary` and a new lint, `record_boundary`, and changes no other lint's findings on any run: on the farm the two arms differ by 15 new `grant_boundary` hits, 5 `record_boundary` hits, and 16 `grant_boundary` findings whose message gained a second reason (2,260 to 2,280 findings).

- `grant_boundary`, line-order drift (X6 RVTAQT-01): in a list where at least a third of the entries open with a sponsor or number line and carry the title on a later line, a run of entries that each open with a title and then a sponsor or number line, starting right after an entry that names a sponsor or number but no title. The titleless entry and the run are flagged. The rule first took the first entry's order as the list's shape, as the label shape does, and missed RVTAQT, whose first grant is a whole record written title-first; with any entry setting the shape and no titleless entry required, it also flagged that first grant, a false positive. Requiring the titleless entry removed it. Over the 306 files it adds 40 hits and removes none, on 11 runs: RVTAQT 306-319 (6) and its re-runs AQCLHS, GINAAP and JJUQDF (6 each, identical entries; AQCLHS-01 lists all six), RXYBVF 482-499 (7), CTWLTR 176 (CTWLTR-01), the titleless entry before the IEUPKK drift (304; IEUPKK-01 lists it) and its re-runs ZCTARO, KUUKNJ and RBHRFR, and web205 497/499 and 547/549 (`_autopsy_artifacts`). The web205 hits are a different cut from the RVTAQT drift: in each, one grant is split into a titleless sponsor-and-period entry and an entry that opens with that grant's title. The render shows it: the sponsor's grant has an empty project title, and the title renders as a grant of its own with no duration. Every hit was read against the stage-4 list; RXYBVF was also read against the source document's line order: its numbered grants put the title on the line after the number, and from grant 32 on stage 2 paired each title with the next number. That makes RXYBVF 502, the one W3B-GR counted false, the last title of that drift, so it is now counted true. 40 of 40 new hits (100%). RVTAQT and IEUPKK are the X6 shape it was written from (in-sample); RXYBVF, CTWLTR and web205 are not.
- `record_boundary`, new, WARN, reads stage 4: outside the grant lists, an entry after the first that opens with a labelled line ("Current position:", "Description:") that at least two, and a third, of its list's other entries carry after their first line, and twice as many as open with it. Over the 306 files: 26 hits on 10 runs, every one read against the stage-4 list. 17 are one mentee list in four runs of one CV (IEUPKK, ZCTARO, KUUKNJ, RBHRFR: a mentee's "Current position:" line opens the next mentee's entry; IEUPKK-07, ZCTARO-02), in-sample. The other 9 are out of sample and each is a record's labelled tail cut into its own entry: a funding line (one CV's research-experience list), a course's description and a course's location (WWSEWY 782, 832), a talk's symposium line split from its title (KHXOUF 777), a patent's inventors (BYFQBG 127), a teaching role's course and duties cut from its role-and-term line (web052 209), and a mentee's current position cut into its own entry (web052 264, web199 574). 26 of 26 (100%), 17 of them from one CV. It does not feed the score.
- Tried and not shipped: an unlabelled form of the same drift outside the grant lists, an entry that opens with its institution, location or organization value when the entry before it lacks that field (IEUPKK 113, 924). Over the first measurement's 221 files it fired 57 times; a hand-read of the distinct hits found mostly whole records written place-first beside date-first ones, or an institution line that heads several roles. Restricted to lists whose entries open with a date, it lost IEUPKK 113 and still fired on 10 other entries, 6 of them one CV's institution lines that each head several dated roles (WYMVGU and IZJADE 59, 70, 77), which are records, not a drift. VPMMFM-03 drifts every entry of its list the same way, so no entry differs from its siblings; only the orphan institution row at its end shows, which `junk_or_header_row` already flags.

| X6-cite | 2026-10-06 | origin/dev `05966dac` plus `citation_field_dropped` and `etal_added` at WARN, against `05966dac`, both over the same stored artifacts (`run_doctor` per run; `scripts/doctor_gate.py` with `--source-dir` for the farm) | 174: the 111 runs under `analysis/<uid>` with stage-4 JSON and a stage-6 docx, each over its stored docx (the 13 under `analysis/pilot/<uid>/outputs` keep a flat layout the harness did not read, so they were not scored there; all 13 CVs are in the farm) (this includes the 6 X6 runs, the 30 NDMRSO runs and the 9 EOAHMI runs), and the 63-run EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm over its base render (`~/worktrees/eb-farm/base`) | the X6 per-run autopsies (`analysis/<uid>/autopsy_verified.json`), the EOAHMI recheck, and the 62 labelled farm runs, as M1 | X6 E3, KJJVVO-10, UXBHHF-20, VPMMFM-08; #1259 |

X6-cite asked what the X6 batch's citation damage needs from the doctor. Its E3 class (95 author lists cut to six and "et al." on all 6 runs: RVTAQT-03, IEUPKK-10, UXBHHF-07, VPMMFM-05, KJJVVO-04, RINASX-16) was already found, record by record, by `etal_added`: 95 of 95 on the stored docx, and on those 6 runs the lint fires on nothing else. What it lacked was weight: it was INFO, and all six runs scored GREEN. It is now WARN. The INFO reasons no longer hold: #1404 (dev-248 onward) took the "first 6, et al." rule out of the 5d prompt, and on the 9 EOAHMI runs built on dev-248 (951 paired citations) `etal_added` fires 0 times, against 94 of the other 102 runs. A hit on a new run is lost co-authors, not the old systemic cut. Severity is all that changed: base and branch have the same `etal_added` findings on every run (1,226 on the 111 runs, 706 on the farm). Since 2026-10-07 it feeds the content-loss cap at 3 or more hits on a run (see the cap section).

A second lint that compares stage 4's author list with the rendered author list name by name was measured and not shipped. On the 6 X6 runs it caught 94 of the 95 E3 lists, one fewer than `etal_added` (RINASX 370, whose stage-4 author list holds initials only), and added 25 hits with no "et al.", all false: 18 PubMed rebuilds that print PubMed's spelling of a name the CV misspells, and 7 lines where 5d printed a name the source wrote as "Firstname Lastname" or with a degree, as "Lastname F".

`citation_field_dropped` is new: a field that identifies the item, left out of its own bibliography line. Base and branch differ in exactly its findings, on every run: 30 on 18 of the 111 runs, 11 on 6 of the farm's 63, all INFO except `title_dropped`. No other lint's finding, severity or count moved, except `etal_added`'s severity. Every hit was read against the stage-4 entry and the rendered docx (`w:t` text with `w:ins`, `w:delText` read apart), and the URL hits also against the docx's hyperlink relationships (none in any of the 8 documents):

- `title_dropped` (WARN): none of stage 4's title words, less the words its venue or other fields hold, is on the line, and no bibliography line holds half of them. 4 hits on the 111 runs, 1 on the farm, all true: KJJVVO 60 (KJJVVO-10, an untitled bulletin item rendered as venue, date and pages), LOOTTE 606 and VYRDHN 607 (LOOTTE-02, VYRDHN-01: 5d formatted one citation for a two-record entry and the second record's title is gone), and QNZADH 308 on both renders (QNZADH-02: a PubMed title cut at inline markup to its first word and a letter; the full title survives only as `w:delText`). 4 of 4 distinct entries (the farm hit is QNZADH 308 again).
- `url_dropped` (INFO): stage 4's `url` is a web address the source also gives, its host is not on the line, and the line has no DOI, PMID or year;volume: locator. 11 hits on 8 of the 111 runs, 2 on the farm, all true: webinars, blog posts, guideline infographics, a toolkit and an e-library item whose only locator was the link (UXBHHF 269 is UXBHHF-20; GKAQHB 269 is the same CV's earlier run). The host appears nowhere in those documents. The 2 farm hits are SEKQUI 537 and SJWASY 431 again: 11 of 11 distinct entries. Before the locator and DOI rules it also fired on 10 lines that print a DOI (preprints, data sets, articles) and 1 journal article with volume and pages, where the link is redundant; before the host rule, on a `url` holding free text.
- `elision_dropped` (INFO): the source elides authors ("...", "[...]", "et al.,") before its title begins, and the line, which is not a PubMed or Crossref rebuild (no PMID or DOI), shows no elision, so the cut list reads as complete. 15 hits on 6 of the 111 runs, 8 on the farm, all true: VPMMFM 410 and 412 (VPMMFM-08) and the same abstracts on the same CV's other runs (CAOACN, KYOPUV, WTKSYX, which adds 408), NJIKGI's five "[...]" lists and QZWBKQ 394. The 8 farm hits are KYOPUV 410/412, NJIKGI's five and QZWBKQ 394 again: 15 of 15 distinct entries, 14 of them on 2 CVs. Without the title-position rule it also fired on an ellipsis in a venue name and on a second citation fused onto an entry; without the PMID/DOI rule, on PubMed rebuilds that rightly print the whole list.

Recall on X6: KJJVVO-10 and UXBHHF-20 caught; VPMMFM-08 2 of its 3 records (408 renders the elision mid-list, "et al.," followed by more names, which reads as elided). The matched lower bound on the farm: 1 of 11 (QNZADH-02); the unmatched 10 are the url and elision hits above, which the farm's autopsies did not list. Every shape was written from these runs, so the precision is in-sample; NDMRSO's 30 and the farm's 63 were read for false positives but the X6 findings drove the shapes. No shape reaches 20 judged hits on distinct CVs, and none feeds the score.

The ledger rows below count each uid and entry once: the farm's 11 hits are all entries the stored runs already hold, so the judged counts are the 30 distinct entries (title 4, url 11, elision 15), not 41. `precision.py` pools a key's shape rows, since a finding carries the lint key and not the shape, so the doctor's label for `citation_field_dropped` reads `p~1.00 n=30` for all three shapes together. That pooled number overstates the WARN shape: `title_dropped` rests on 4 entries on 4 CVs.

| X6-multirec | 2026-10-06 | `feat/doctor-x6-multi-record` on origin/dev `05966dac`, against `05966dac`, both over the same documents (`scripts/doctor_gate.py`; `run_doctor` per run for `analysis/`) | 335: the 111 runs under `analysis/<uid>` with stage-4 JSON and a stored docx (NDMRSO's 30, the 6 X6 runs, EOAHMI, RCBKFG and older batches), each over its stored docx; the 63-run farm over its base render; the 126 farm and batch runs over a fresh render of `05966dac` (no `--source-dir`); NDMRSO's 30 over the `ndm-farm` dev render and EOAHMI's 9 over their dev-248 render | the X6 per-run autopsies (`analysis/<uid>/autopsy_verified.json`, class E1); as M1 on the 62 labelled farm runs | X6 E1, #1243 |

X6-multirec extends `multi_record_coverage` with two shapes, from the X6 batch's E1 findings the lint missed (2026-10-04, dev-246; 12 E1 findings on 6 runs, of which the dev-HEAD lint already caught 8). Each finding of the span shape names it in its message (`[span_outside_record]`).

- `span_outside_record`: a clause whose words the record holds but which writes a year outside every span the record's dates cover: a committee's membership span with an office inside it, where stage 4 kept the office (RINASX-10, 3 entries). On a one-record entry the clause the record's dates stand for must name a role the left-out clause lacks, so the same role written again at another time stays silent, as before. It is also the one shape judged on an entry stage 4 split into `stage4_records`, which the lint used to skip whole (VPMMFM-10: the two offices split out, the membership span lost).
- A dated event beside a named role: in an entry with fewer than two dated clauses, a part that writes a year and two or more distinctive words, none of them the record's, is a clause beside a part that names a title and an institution (RVTAQT-08: a retreat beside a committee membership).

Neither shape judges M2A/M2B/M2C: a grant writes its own period beside the owner's role period, and its credit line beside its award line (BMHBJZ 668, NTCULM 62, web31 455 fired in a first draft). The first draft's dated event also took a part that shares words with the record: 30 more hits, 27 of them false or partial, nearly all a record's own head line beside its detail (a mentee's name and degree year beside "Committee member", a residency's dates beside its director's line, a talk beside its co-presenter; MQSUIC 619-720, TXTATQ 108/113/1081, web08, web190, web199, web200, web202, web32 35/39 and others). Requiring no shared word removes all 30, and with them 3 true hits (KDAZOM 417; web209 86 and 91, an endowed professorship beside a division chief post, which share the department's name). MQSUIC 691 stays: its mentee's name has no word of five letters.

Base and branch differ only in `multi_record_coverage` findings, on every arm: no other lint's finding moved. Added or changed, each read against the stage-4 entry and the rendered docx (`w:t` with `w:ins`, table cells), with a run counted once where two arms hold it:

- `analysis/` (stored docx), 32: the X6 targets RINASX 422/424/435, RVTAQT 63 and VPMMFM 62; the same CVs' earlier runs DTFNOR, EQADVR and UYFRTL 422/424/435, AQCLHS, GINAAP and JJUQDF 63, CAOACN and KYOPUV 62; BMHBJZ 66 (a chief's post fused into the professorship row, its own span lost) and 401 (a chair-elect term lost beside a membership); BNYLDF 289 and 296 (split entries keeping the offices and losing the society membership spans) and 329 (now also the board membership span); KDAZOM 415, SIYORB 296 and VXSDRD 88 (membership span lost, office kept). All true: 27. Partial: HUOGDE 165 (a journal advisory board role dropped from the journal record: a field, not a second record), KDAZOM 608 (an award record losing the mentee paper it was for), NJIKGI 450 (INFO: three advising roles fused into one record, rendered), WYMVGU 941 (INFO: a committee membership inside an entry coded F1). False: MQSUIC 691, a mentee's own head line.
- 63-run farm, 2 not already above: BFSUMA 109 and VQFSDI 378 (membership or board span lost, office kept), both true.
- 126 farm and batch runs, 10: 1FRABQ, CZTMHW and UYZIAG 105 (one CV; a presidency now also reported lost), web163 158 (a staff post lost beside a directorship), web205 254, web228 166 (a membership span and a treasurer term lost), web32 1106, 1108 and 1132 (member, vice-chair and chair fused into one record dated by the membership; the offices' spans render nowhere): 9 true. web46 387 is partial (a mentee line coded D1; the mentee's name is what is lost).
- NDMRSO dev render and EOAHMI dev-248 render: only runs already listed (BNYLDF, HUOGDE, SIYORB, VXSDRD, WYMVGU).

38 true, 5 partial, 1 false of 44 (86%); of the 42 WARNs, 38 (90%). 19 of the true hits are the X6 targets and the same CVs' other runs, so those are in-sample. Out of sample the WARNs are 19 true of 23 by run, but 1FRABQ, CZTMHW and UYZIAG are one CV: counted once per CV, about 17 true of 21 (81%), at the 80% bar with n about 21. No base finding was removed: the five base findings whose message changed (BNYLDF 329, 1FRABQ/CZTMHW/UYZIAG 105, web228 166) gained a clause each, every one true. On the 62 labelled runs `multi_record_coverage` goes from 152 hits (103 WARN, matched 85, caught 48) to 165 (115 WARN, matched 90, caught 51), and recall from 261 to 263 of 487.

Recall on X6's 12 E1 findings, on the stored docx: 11 caught. 8 already were on origin/dev (IEUPKK-04, -05; UXBHHF-03, -04, -05; KJJVVO-02, -03; VPMMFM-01, which dev-246 missed and RCB-D's apostrophe years catch); X6-multirec adds RINASX-10 (all 3 entries), RVTAQT-08 and VPMMFM-10.
