| X6-header | 2026-10-06, re-run 2026-10-07 | origin/dev `05966dac` plus `group_header_context`; re-run against origin/dev `36f56eb6`, base and branch over the same renders (`scripts/doctor_gate.py` with `--source-dir`) | 210: the 111 stored runs under `analysis/<uid>` with stage-4 JSON and a stage-6 docx (the live documents; NDMRSO's 30 and X6's 6 among them), the 63-run EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm over its render of `fb466a0f` (`eb-farm-w3b`), and the NDMRSO (30) and X6 (6) farms over their renders of `05966dac` (the re-run reuses these renders: a doctor-only change) | the X6 per-run autopsies (`analysis/<uid>/autopsy_verified.json`, E8 and E11); as M1 on the 62 labelled farm runs | X6 E8 (12 findings on 6 runs), E11 (RVTAQT-02, RINASX-14), #985 |

X6-header adds `group_header_context`: a CV's group header whose context never reached the rows of the lines under it. `junk_or_header_row` reports the header row itself and skips I (a society line), and `section_consistency` reads codes against headings and text, not against the list under a line; neither looks at what the lines under a header lost. Each finding names its shape:

- `children_lost_header` (WARN): an organization or institution alone (as `header_only` reads it, dated only by a span), followed under its heading by lines of the same family (society I/Q1-Q3, employer D/G/L/O/P, teaching K) that name no holder, whose rows show under half of the header's name. Under a dated header only bare roles count: a dated society line is a membership of its own, and a committee after it may be another body's (MUHLLD 82, 84, one flat membership list). Before that rule it also fired on 18 dated headers.
- `role_without_holder` (WARN): a line that is a role and its dates only ('Chair 2010-2011', 'Course Director'), with a role word, no ',', 'of', 'for', 'on', 'in' or 'at', and at most three other words, rendered as a row of the role alone. The finding names the line above the run when that line can hold the roles (it names something besides its dates and holds no role of its own: RINASX 29, a course over 30-34), and otherwise the run's first role, quoting the roles after it so no entry is named twice. The line above can be a date alone (RGUNJV 2987, a T line) or a sibling role line (DTFNOR 31), and an earlier version named it as the holder either way; the version after that always named the first role, which dropped RINASX 29 from the finding and quoted 30 twice. The first version, without those limits, fired 88 times on the `analysis` runs, mostly on O rows whose `leadership_role` holds the whole line ('Chair, <committee> ...').
- `header_coded_unlike_list` (INFO): an undated line of 12 words or fewer, no list item and no stated role, followed by 3 dated entries sharing one code of another section letter, and rendered as a row of its own.
- `parent_dates_lost` (INFO): an undated D or K role at an institution directly under a dated block of its letter (an entry stage 2 joined from several lines, or a dated group header), rendered with no year. The first version read any dated line above as a block and fired on undated source lines in a list (RNKYST 18-21, SEKQUI 95/97, WWSEWY 27/28) and on an entry whose only date is a month and a two-digit year (QZWBKQ 45).

Base and branch differ only in this lint's findings, on every run of every arm, with nothing removed. Against origin/dev `36f56eb6` (2026-10-07): `analysis` 4,075 to 4,260 (+142 WARN, +43 INFO, on 46 of 111 runs), EBYSBC farm 1,915 to 1,985 (+55, +15, 23 of 63), NDMRSO 810 to 867 (+48, +9, of which one INFO is the lint's 'skipped: missing stage_6_docx' notice on QFQLNF, not a hit: 8 INFO hits), X6 209 to 225 (+8, +8). The added findings are the same as against `05966dac` (3,775 to 3,960; 1,770 to 1,840; 768 to 825; 178 to 194); only the base grew. The NDMRSO and X6 dev renders give the same (run, entry, shape) set as the stored documents of those runs; the farm render adds 4 its `analysis` runs lack (PFBSNH 81 and 82, TXTATQ 895, ZGLAAD 127). Every hit on the `analysis` runs and those 4 was read by one reviewer against the stage-4 entries, the stage-2 neighbours where the grouping was in doubt, and the rendered row the finding quotes (table rows and body paragraphs, `w:ins` text included):

- `children_lost_header`: 109 hits, 105 true: society and employer headers whose committees, offices and memberships render with an empty organization cell (KJJVVO-01's five societies, the same CV's three other runs, CXRYCF, BNYLDF, KHXOUF and QTATUP, IZJADE and WYMVGU), and institution lines over course lists (OIYKZE, BZZNRL, VGHNZD 255's 16 courses). 1 false: KHXOUF 106, a city line read as the employer of the next post. 3 partial: UYQRUN 176 (the child row names the university, not the college), SDEBQJ 354 and 358 ('SCHOOL', 'CAMPUS': a level, not a body). 96%.
- `role_without_holder`: 33 hits, 29 true (RINASX-06 and its two other runs, offices under a society or a committee, a dean's terms under the college line, 'Member' lines under a society); 4 partial: ZQJVRN 226 and 247, 'Research Mentor' lines that lead a mentee list rather than follow a course, and IZJADE and WYMVGU 479, a dean's term in a list of administrative posts with no line above that names the school (first read as true; re-read for X6-header-cap). 88%. The finding's subject moved twice, its count never: the same 55 role findings (33 on `analysis`) on every arm, each with the same severity. On 2026-10-07 it names the line above when that line can hold the roles (15 findings, each read: a course over its roles, a society over its offices, the college line over its deans) and otherwise the run's first role (40), quoting the roles after it; no finding names an entry twice. A dated committee line (DUTAVD 78, PFBSNH 82, BZZNRL 124) is not named as the holder: it is a membership of its own, and the offices after it can be its society's.
- `header_coded_unlike_list`: 22 hits, 19 true (IEUPKK-18's two lines in each of the CV's four runs, RINASX-14, GCFEBE 268, a 'Research Mentor' over mentees, institution lines coded as committees over their committees, society lines coded I over their offices, which KJJVVO-01 counts as part of the defect); 2 false: VVRTUC 265 (an expert panel, a record, before a list of talks) and DUTAVD 60 (a membership before a run-in heading); 1 partial: UYQRUN 157, whose quoted row is another entry's. 86%. On the farm: 7 of 9 (ZGLAAD 127, a membership whose lines name the society themselves, is false).
- `parent_dates_lost`: 21 hits, 21 true, but on two CVs only: IEUPKK-14 and IEUPKK-17 in the CV's four runs, and SEKQUI 218. n is small and one CV dominates.

The WARN shapes together: 134 of 142 true (94%), at or above the 80% bar on more than 20; the INFO shapes 40 of 43. Some runs are the same CV run more than once (IEUPKK, KUUKNJ, RBHRFR and ZCTARO; KJJVVO, UPFAYD, ZDCXIV and FLYBMX; IZJADE and WYMVGU), so hits repeat across them. Every shape was written from these runs, so the precision is in-sample; the next batch is the first out-of-sample check. Recall on X6 (dev render): E8 9 of 12 (RVTAQT-06, RVTAQT-07, UXBHHF-09 and VPMMFM-14 were already `junk_or_header_row`'s; this adds IEUPKK-14, all 5 records; IEUPKK-17; IEUPKK-18, both lines; KJJVVO-01, 7 headers and 23 of its other records, against 2 before; RINASX-06, all 5 roles) and the two E11 targets (RVTAQT-02 was already `section_consistency`'s; this adds RINASX-14). Not caught: IEUPKK-16 (a sub-heading's words lost from a training row whose title stage 4 filled with the qualification), UXBHHF-10 (a lab named in place of the employer its siblings carry in their own text) and VPMMFM-11 (a board journal on a continuation line coded Q4D). On the 62 labelled farm runs, recall rises from 197 to 206 of 487 against `05966dac`, and from 204 to 212 against `36f56eb6` (+8, against +9 before; which finding the newer base already matched was not traced).

The EBYSBC verdicts were given on the doctor deployed for that batch (dev-242, `f4f087fc`). On M1's code every judged (run, lint) pair still fires, so the verdict columns describe the same findings as the hit columns.

| FRAG | 2026-10-07 | `fix/1256-merge-fragment-text` on origin/dev `f103edac`: `lint_orphaned_fragments` run directly over every stored stage-3b JSON as stored (the doctor these runs get today), and again after `stage3b.fragment_merge.merge_fragment_text` has run over it (runs built after #1256) | 125 distinct stored stage-3b JSON files (content-deduplicated): the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot, NDMRSO, X6 and EOAHMI farms and `analysis/<uid>` | every one of the corpus's 48 `is_fragment` entries read against its neighbours by hand; the pilot autopsy (JNATFN 262, BFSUMA 189) and NDMRSO ND12 (MQJAVH 201) | #1256, #1365 |

FRAG adds `orphaned_fragments`: a stage-3b fragment whose text is not in the text of the entry it belongs to. Stage 4 skips every fragment, so the line reaches no record. WARN, or INFO when stage 3b leaves the line out on purpose (a header, column label or page stamp the fragment pass's reasoning names, or an organisation sub-heading next to a coded record). Reads stage 3b only.

As stored: 41 hits on 22 of 125 runs, 18 WARN and 23 INFO. Of the 18 WARNs, 16 are true (lost content: a title tail, an assignee, four examiner sessions, a location, poster dates, a trial's drug name, a mentee's current position, a numbered item) and 2 partial (ZGLAAD 137/138, an institution line pair pointing at each other: context, not a record). The 23 INFOs are all headers, column labels, year or drug-name dividers and organisation sub-headings, as labelled. After the merge: 29 hits on 17 runs, 6 WARN (WWSEWY 539/543, RBHRFR 468, RWBQKF 94, ZGLAAD 137/138) and the same 23 INFO. In-sample: the merge's skip rules were written from these runs.

| OIE-SUM | 2026-10-07 | `fix/1554-summary-claims-lint` on origin/dev `cdd578f9`: `lint_summary_unsupported_claim` run directly over each run's stored stage-4.5 and stage-4 JSON (and the source docx where the run kept one) | the 14 OIEPQD runs (dev-258, after #1542), and for false positives the 107 stored stage-4.5 summaries of the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot (63), NDMRSO (29), EOAHMI (9) and X6 (6) farms | the OIEPQD per-run autopsies' stage-4.5 rows | #1554 |

OIE-SUM adds `summary_unsupported_claim`: a sentence of the generated research summary that claims a pending application with no pending grant in stage 4, funding with no grant, mentoring with no N-coded entry or mentoring words, or a funder no entry or source line names. WARN, one per claim kind, no score cap. On OIEPQD it fires 11 times on 9 runs, each on a sentence an autopsy verified (pending: BMYYOX, GGWLYS, PZTNWT, TVQZIP, UJNBIT; funding: PZTNWT; mentoring: BMYYOX, CULCSV, GOWQKA, XACIVX; funder: YYVITM). It misses, by construction, claims of a kind the CV does hold: UNKNBU's old letter of intent called current (the CV has M2C entries), ATQWCA's foundation award called NIH-funded (the CV names NIH elsewhere), BMYYOX's study credited to the wrong funder, and inflated mentoring on VECUUM and XAYKXA (both list some). On the farms its first draft fired 6 times; 3 were false (a CDC spelled "Center for", a study-section sentence opening "Funding", and mentoring words in abstracts at a trainee symposium that the draft did not count), each fixed. The shipped lint fires 3 times there (AKPQEB, AQCLHS, HZGJFM), each a mentoring claim on a CV whose stage-4 entries and source docx list none (AKPQEB lists resident teaching, which the OIEPQD autopsies did not count as mentoring). 14 of 14 hand-checked, in-sample: the rules were written from these runs.

| Y1585 | 2026-10-08 | `fix/1585-doctor-year-span-fp` on origin/dev `2cae24d7`, against `2cae24d7`, both over the same stored renders (`scripts/doctor_gate.py`) | 278: the 37 YUYVIG runs over their dev-259 render (with `--source-dir` where the run kept a docx source, 15), and held out, the 112 runs under `analysis/<uid>` with stage-4 JSON over their stored docx (`--source-dir`, 111), the 66 farm runs in `src/unified_pipeline/outputs` (no `--source-dir`) and the 63-run EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm (`~/worktrees/eb-farm/base`, `--source-dir`) | the YUYVIG per-run verifier verdicts (`_labels_yuyvig`, 37 runs, 169 indexed findings), and as M1 | #1585 |

Y1585 changes `year_not_in_source` and `span_count` and no other lint, on every run of the four sets: base and branch differ only in findings of those two lints, and only by findings removed. `year_not_in_source` reads four more two-digit year shapes, in `_year_in_source` only (implausible_year's `_year_written` is unchanged): a dash-joined m-d-yy date ("3-30-00", "2-28- 86"), the later two-digit tails of a list ("2014,15,16", "98-00,02,04"), a two-digit year after a month word that no comma or four-digit year follows ("Oct. 98"), and one opening an open range ("16- present"). `span_count` takes a comma straight after a month or term word with the word ("June, 2018"), and joins two lone source years whose gap holds a dash with no year on its other side ("2007– Professor of X, Y<tab>2012", "2014 ... to 2019"), unless the later year opens a dash of its own; the join reads the source only, since a rendered "2011 - Title 2013 - Title" line is a list.

On YUYVIG, 62 findings go, every one a false positive the batch's verifier named in #1585: `year_not_in_source` 49 to 9 (XNWSZN 34, SIJYJZ 4, ECXGAT 20, TYGUXX 319), and `span_count` 28 to 6 (BLBVPD 8, TQXZNI 6, FMIGLR 5, LTTYWI 2, HNQBOI 16). All 5 `year_not_in_source` true positives still fire (XNWSZN 1120, IZABPD 332, XVSQFF 37, UVZNIC 559 and 592), and so do all 6 `span_count` ones (RTBEJA 114, 115 and 116, SLYBST 96, SIJYJZ 143, ZDOAZO 221). The 4 `year_not_in_source` false positives left are out of scope in #1585: two source typos (OKRTPJ 888, UVZNIC 893), a year on a child line (FMIGLR 282) and RLADNC 1920. Scored against the labels, recall goes from 61 to 58 of 169: the 3 lost are HNQBOI-06, -07 and -08, which the base matched only through the `span_count` false positive on entry 16 (the verifier's report puts HNQBOI-08's range on entry 17, which neither arm flags). The shapes were written from YUYVIG, so these figures are in-sample.

Held out: on the 112 `analysis` runs, 2 findings go, both false: BNYLDF 394 (`year_not_in_source`, "Nov 92-Feb 1993", start 1992) and OIYKZE 420 (`span_count`, the cell-split range RCB-SC already counted false). The EBYSBC farm loses only OIYKZE 420 again (the same CV), so its scores do not move (recall 272 of 487 both arms); the 66 farm runs do not change. No `span_count` hit RCB-SC counted true is lost on these sets. A first version that joined wrapped ranges on the rendered line too added 3 false hits there (GKAQHB and RWBQKF 89, VNUAHA 623) and read "May 13," as a year (WWSEWY 880); the shipped rules add none. The year shapes were then narrowed so a number before a dash that opens a title ("Volume 20 - Title"), a month day a word follows ("May 13 at") and a wrapped-range gap holding a semicolon or a dash-comma no longer vouch, and the list-tail shape now reads a tail after a range shorthand ("2005-06, 16"); over all four sets the findings are identical to the looser version's. A dash-joined number run that reads as an m-d-yy date ("room 4-10-10") still vouches. Not re-run: RCB-SC's 13 pilot runs and the 60 `_autopsy_artifacts` batch runs. The `span_count` row below is RCB-SC's 118 hits less OIYKZE 420, plus YUYVIG's 6 (123 hits, 117 true); none of the 6 matches a YUYVIG label, so matched and caught stay at RCB-SC's 4 and 2.

| N1585 | 2026-10-13 | `fix/1585-doctor-date-shapes` on origin/dev `b7654ef9`, against `b7654ef9`, `run_doctor` per run, both arms over the same stored artifacts (with the run's source docx where it kept one) | 502 stored stage-4 artifacts: the 40 NECRGC/JAFPFP and 39 b13 runs under `~/worktrees/*/analysis`, 297 under the main checkout's `analysis/` (133 run dirs, their S3 copies and replay farms), the 66-run `src/unified_pipeline/outputs` farm and the 60 `_autopsy_artifacts` batch runs of 2026-09-11 and 2026-09-17 | the NECRGC per-run verifier verdicts quoted in #1585 | #1585 |

N1585 reads two more source date shapes. Both `year_not_in_source` (`_year_in_source`) and `date_cell_shape`'s open marker read a hyphen, non-breaking hyphen, figure dash or en dash as "-" (`doctor/shared.py` `normalise_hyphens`), so "1971\u201174" states 1974 and a trailing "2015\u2011" is open. `year_not_in_source` also reads a range of two m-yy dates joined by "to", "through", "thru", "until" or a spaced dash ("6-91 to 5-94"); each month must be 1-12, and an unspaced run ("3-24-11-26") still states no year. 3 of 502 runs change, and only by findings removed: NJHLUR (`year_not_in_source` 39 to 1, `date_cell_shape` WARN 9 to 0), WBZIDP (`year_not_in_source` 3 to 0) and YIXQFB (`year_not_in_source` 11 to 0). These are the 61 findings the NECRGC verifiers judged false; NJHLUR 469, the one true `year_not_in_source` hit on these runs, still fires. No other lint changes on any run. The shapes were written from these runs, so the figures are in-sample. Left on #1585: `implausible_year` on YIXQFB's wrong-century stage-4 values that render right (2642, 2664, 2669, 2674, 2684; 2743 is true and fires), and `duplicate_records`.

| S1554-PC | 2026-10-14 | `fix/1554-summary-unsupported-claims` on origin/dev `89f26a94`, against `89f26a94`: `lint_summary_unsupported_claim` run directly over each run's stored stage-4.5 and stage-4 JSON (no source lines) | 79 labelled: the 39 YUYVIG runs under `~/worktrees/cviche-autopsy-b13/analysis` (37 labelled) and the 40 NECRGC/JAFPFP runs under `~/worktrees/cviche-autopsy-necrgc/analysis`; and 224 distinct (uid, summary) pairs with no labelled uid: the main checkout's `analysis/`, the eb, ndm, x6, eo and wave1 farms and `src/unified_pipeline/outputs` | the #1554 YUYVIG table (19 verified summary defects, 18 clean runs) and NECRGC table (14 defects), matched by sentence | #1554 |

S1554-PC adds four per-claim checks to `summary_unsupported_claim`, each at INFO: a status word the named grant's own end date or heading contradicts (`grant_status`), a declined award counted as held (`not_received_award`), a funder the named grant's line does not name (`funder_link`), and a lead-role claim its role does not give (`lead_role`). A sentence names a grant by a run of its title words, or, for a status word near a funding word, by its agency. It also stops two mentoring false positives of the presence check: advising or supervising students, and an M1 line the owner mentored ("Faculty mentored project with resident"), now count as mentoring.

On the 79 labelled runs the base fires 2 WARN, both false (HNQBOI and HBALTD, mentoring); the branch fires none of them, and 12 INFO on 12 runs. By sentence, 9 are true: ZDOAZO-01, ATRKVV-02, WPJHYT-05, SPINJC-05, RLADNC-10, FMIGLR-07, FCAAUV-07, UAURFU-05 (the sentence before the labelled one, naming the same ended grant) and OKRTPJ-13 (partial: its "completed" items, not its role verb). 3 are false: IZIXVF ("Current projects examine" a topic whose grant ended before the CV), HNQBOI (a curriculum "completed with support" whose grant line has no date) and EYTHNB ("I lead" a work group whose name a grant title shares). Recall: 7 of the 9 YUYVIG HIGH/MED defects (DYLJXC-16 is out of reach: stage 4's own agency line names the network; XVSQFF-08's claim names no grant), 8 of all 19, and 1 of the 14 NECRGC defects. Of the 18 YUYVIG clean runs, it fires on IZIXVF only. In-sample: the rules were written from these runs.

On the 224 other summaries, WARN is unchanged (27 findings, the same on both arms). INFO adds 15 on 14 runs. Read by hand, not by a verifier: 7 look true (MOFVYH, KDAZOM, WIANVH twice, UXBHHF, RWBQKF, WTKSYX), 3 false (VB4VY7, GINAAP, web177), and 5 follow the rule's letter but are unconfirmed: a status word on a grant whose line gives no dates (FLYBMX, BMHBJZ, UNKNBU, KHXOUF) and NIH named over a grant whose line names no agency (RGUNJV). These summaries are mostly third-person ones from before #1558. They were also used to remove 13 false positives while the rules were written, and 7 unseen scratch-farm summaries to remove 1 more, so neither set is held out any longer.

| D1585 | 2026-10-15 | `fix/1585-doctor-dup-records-fp` on origin/dev `75d4fc6b`, against `75d4fc6b`: `lint_duplicate_records` and `lint_implausible_year` run directly over each run's stored stage-4 and stage-5d JSON and its stored docx (`read_docx_blocks`), both arms over the same files | 241 stored runs with a stage-4 JSON and a rendered docx, one per uid: 162 under the main checkout's `analysis/` and `src/unified_pipeline/outputs`, the 39 YUYVIG runs and the 40 NECRGC/JAFPFP runs | the YUYVIG and NECRGC verifier verdicts quoted in #1585 | #1585 |

D1585 changes `duplicate_records` and `implausible_year` only. `duplicate_records`' record rule no longer pairs two citations that carry two different PMIDs (one paper co-published in two journals, a preprint and its paper), and a pair whose copies are two source entries with spans that do not overlap, each of whose own source text states the title, is the CV listing the record twice: it moves to a separate INFO finding ("the source CV itself lists twice"), out of the WARN count and the score. A copy whose source heading names abstracts while it is coded S1-S3 keeps the pair at WARN (the recode made it). `implausible_year` takes the docx as an optional view and does not report a wrong-century year the page never prints in four digits (stage 6 writes a grant duration as mm/yy). On 241 runs: `duplicate_records` WARN pairs 141 on 61 runs to 50 on 26; 86 pairs on 41 runs move to INFO, and 5 pairs go (YIXQFB 2980/2981, 3201/3207, 3203/3208, RGUNJV 421/463 a preprint and its paper, YME2VA 694/699 two Mol Ther issues). `implausible_year` WARN 20 to 14: YIXQFB 2642, 2664, 2669, 2674, 2684, and held out CTWLTR 128 (end 1912 for "4/30/2012", rendered "05/09-04/12"); YIXQFB 2743 ("1909-1908" on the page) still fires, and the 10 INFO ceiling findings do not change. YUY-OA adds `owner_attribution`: other people's content rendered as the CV owner's. It changes no other lint's findings on any of the 145 runs, and adds 57 findings on 36 of them. Two shapes:

- `mentee_under_non_mentee_heading` (WARN): N3, N3A or N3B entries under a leaf heading, or under the nearest undated, unnumbered lead line of the same heading path, that names review, applicants, a panel, staff, technicians or lab managers and names no trainee, thesis or dissertation. 3 findings, 3 true: ECXGAT 1293-1299 (ECXGAT-03, the 4 grant-review applicants coded N3B; the lead line survives only as entry 1288, since stage 1b placed no headings), IZIXVF 284-305 (IZIXVF-01; 22 staff, 284-298 N3B and 299-305 N3), and DYLJXC 403, a person and thesis title under a "Reviewer:" lead line, below the entries the source marks as research done in the owner's laboratory; no autopsy row names it. Neither autopsy row matches by index (ECXGAT-03's label lists 1288 and 2104; IZIXVF-01's lists none), so matched is 0 of 3.
- `citation_without_owner`: a run of bibliography publications (every S code but S0 and S9) whose stage-4 authors and source never name the owner, exactly or, in the source, one letter off a long surname. A group or committee credit in the source, an agency or academy as author, or no stage-4 authors is not judged. An elided source ("et al.") joins a run but cannot start a finding, and a credited citation ends the run. WARN when the run sits under a heading or lead line naming acknowledgements or citing; INFO otherwise. WARN: 1 of 1 true, ECXGAT 2156-2204 (ECXGAT-01): 18 publications, not the issue's 19, because 2186 rendered as a PubMed rebuild whose line does not pair with its source (`CITATION_MATCH_MIN_SHARE`). INFO: 53 findings on 33 runs, each read against its source entry: 12 true (talks given by a student, postdoc or resident, abstracts and papers with a complete author list that omits the owner, and OKRTPJ 408-427, whose tail entry 427 is OKRTPJ-03, the owner's citation split so the rendered part lost them), 41 false: the owner under another surname (ATUVAL 6, MRJDWE and UXBHHF 4 each, QQGKXR 2, SIJYJZ), trial or consortium papers listing no owner (RLADNC 2 runs of 107 and 16, BMAMWE 2, ULHCAP 2, RGUNJV 2, CYOFWJ, OKRTPJ 487), agency, academy or edited reports (MVUREJ 2, RGUNJV 2, WYMVGU, IZJADE), the owner's talks given with others (DPEHSZ, MQJAVH, YOXXOH, VGHNZD), a garbled author list (BLBVPD), an owner named by first name (XVSQFF), and NDXXAD 197 and VXSDRD 483. 12 of 53 (23%) is under the 80% WARN bar, so this half ships INFO, as #1573 asked.

Recall: YUYVIG 61 to 63 of 169 located findings (ECXGAT-01, OKRTPJ-03); the 62 labelled farm runs 204 to 205 of 487. On the farm both matches are index overlaps with other defects: NDXXAD-04 (entry 197, a paper the owner annotates as a contributor, counted false above), the one new catch, and ZDCXIV-10 (entry 109, whose source the autopsy says names no owner), which another lint already caught. The shapes were written from these runs, so the precision is in-sample. Not reached: ULHCAP-01 (a trainee's award under a publications heading) and DYLJXC-03 (a host's title read as the owner's role).

YUY-HO is the first held-out measurement: every row above scores lints on the runs they were written from. The YUYVIG verifiers judged the doctor the batch ran (dev-259), so the held-out precision in the table under "Held-out precision (YUY-HO)" is their WARN verdicts on that doctor: 278 of 396 (70%). Four doctor PRs merged on 2026-10-08 were written from these same runs, so what they change here is in-sample. #1607 (#1585) removes 62 false positives (`year_not_in_source` 49 to 9 hits, `span_count` 28 to 6). #1616 adds `owner_attribution`, 4 WARN, all true by YUY-OA's reading. #1611 turns 4 more `dedup_drops` findings to WARN. #1603 (#1590) changes no YUYVIG count. On the current doctor that is about 282 of 338 WARN true (83%), not re-judged; the 7 `dedup_drops` WARNs are not re-judged either.

Labels, v2. The v1 labels (`_labels_yuyvig`, which Y1585 and YUY-OA scored) read a finding's entry indices from a 3-line window starting at its first line. On a one-line finding the window reaches into the next findings: of the 141 indices v2 drops from 83 v1 findings, 138 belong to another finding of the same run. v2 (`_labels_yuyvig_v2`, built by `_yuyvig_autopsy/labels_v2.py`; both local, beside the v1 labels) reads each finding's own block. It takes indices only from entry anchors ("entry 34-39", "element_idx_start 287", "275 (Q4C)", a table cell's leading list), and keeps a number only if it starts an entry in that run's stage-2, 3b or 4 JSON. It indexes 250 of the 444 (v1: 169). It also records the stage the verifier blamed first (`stage`) and the issue it cites (`class_ref`). 68 findings the verifier marked NEW are re-keyed to the issue filed on 2026-10-08 that cites their id (`remap_new.py`). Each report's WARN verdicts are transcribed by hand into `doctor_review` (`reviews.py`; the process notice `llm_fallback_served` left out, as the ECXGAT verifier did). Matched counts on v1 and v2 are not comparable. `scripts/doctor_vs_autopsy.py` now groups recall by `stage` as well.

The render stubs the LLM (`render_gate.py`), as every render gate does. On the 36 runs with a stored report, every lint's hit count matches that report except: the lints #1607, #1611 and #1616 change (above); three stage-6 shapes (`appendix_no_route` 8 to 3 after #1621 routes bare N3, `appendix_m1_not_in_summary` 6 to 8 after #1615, `appendix_passthrough_refused` 1 to 2, IZABPD's J entry); the INFO shape `other` 2 to 7 (#1621's new "bare mentee code resolved" message); `llm_fallback_served` 25 to 12 (the farm has no prompt logs); and `missed_headers` 20 to 19.

- YUYVIG, 32 hits of 7,556 5d citations, in-sample (the rules were written on these runs): 20 true, a name or number the line does not hold: a bare initials token merged into the next author (FLBFRK 25), initials given to authors the line gives none or fewer (BCTOGR 1286, 2958, 4190, 4861, ECXGAT 911, FMIGLR 1414, IXJMKS 604, OKRTPJ 464, SQMWHM 853, XNWSZN 348), initials shifted one author along (QQGKXR 481, 483), an author the line does not name (BCTOGR 1964, 2022, OKRTPJ 490), and an ordinal: the CV's list number (SIJYJZ 732) or a meeting number the line never gives (ECXGAT 859, ULHCAP 668, UVZNIC 1794). 10 partial: 5d spelling out a name the line misspells or garbles (BCTOGR 3048, 3341, 4109, 4119, 4360, 4400; BLBVPD 1785, 1867, 2441; QQGKXR 479). 2 false: ECXGAT 980 (a surname glued to the next word across a two-word surname) and VTRKUF 341 (5d shortened a hyphenated given name to one initial, which drops text rather than adding it).
- Held out, 91 hits on 37 of 238 runs, of 21,951 5d citations: 42 true (initials the line does not give, an author it does not name, a numbered meeting or edition it does not number, a list number read as an ordinal, a generational suffix it does not give), 31 partial (7 spelling fixes; 24 where the line is a fragment of a split citation, a garbled list, a translated meeting name, or the owner's own line under a name `cv_owner` does not carry), 18 false (initials split around the surname or by a date the PDF layout put between them, OCR digits read as letters, a given name glued to the next word, a dropped initial).

Over both sets, 62 of 123 are true (50%), 41 partial and 20 false: under the 80% WARN bar, so the lint ships INFO, and (Paul, 2026-10-08) as no run-page finding at all: the review copy comments each flagged citation as a possibility to check against the CV (`REVIEW_COPY_ONLY_LINTS`), and the score never counts it. The CV owner is exempt (`cv_owner`): crediting the owner on their own line invents nothing. Without the exemption the lint flags 70 more lines (10 YUYVIG, 60 held out), the owner credited on lines such as letters to the editor and talks given "with" a colleague. Recall: YUYVIG 62 to 63 of 169 located findings, one HIGH (FLBFRK 25); the other three #1570 entries carry no `element_idx_start` in the labels.

Why stage 5d does not reject these yet: a replay of 5d from the YUYVIG prompt logs (no paid call; the base arm reproduces all 19,350 stored entries exactly) that dropped each failing reply sent 41 lines to stage 6's stage-4 render, and by a word-overlap count that render lost source words on 23 of them, most often an S8 entry's meeting and place (SIJYJZ 732 kept only its talk title and year, 18 source words lost to remove one). Since #1570's stage-6 fix the stage-4 render prints an S8 entry's `conference_name` and `location` (`Meeting; Year; Place.`). Re-measured on the 20 citations the check flags in the stored dev-259 YUYVIG roll, with each one's pre-5d fields put back: the 14 S8 fallbacks lose 110 source words, down from 202, against 85 for the 5d citations, and 12 of the 14 now name their meeting or place; the other 2 lines hold neither. 10 of the 14 fallbacks still lose more source words than the citation (an abstract number, a month, a presentation type), which is why 5d still does not reject.

YUY-SP adds `shattered_prose`: a stage-4 entry whose `\t` parts break mid-sentence (at least 2 joins and 30% of them, at least one opening lowercase) and at least 3 of which render as whole paragraphs, which is one source paragraph printed one bullet per printed line. It changes no other lint's findings on any of the 272 runs, and adds 15 WARN findings on 3 of them, all true on reading the render: IZABPD 430, 436, 441, 445, 450, 455 (L2), 468, 477, 488 (L3) and RLADNC 530, 540, 567 (L3), which are YUYVIG's IZABPD-02 and RLADNC-04, and the pilot farm's JNATFN 680, 686, 699 (L1, a docx source). Neither YUYVIG label carries an entry index, so matched is 0 by index; by hand the lint catches both. Over the 185 stored runs under `analysis/` (stored renders, mixed images), it also fires on OIEPQD VECUUM 21 (L2), true. A first version without the lowercase-opening rule also fired on wave-1 web228 457, a three-line practice address broken at commas, false. The thresholds were fitted on these runs, so the precision is in-sample. Not reached: the same prose rendered inline as `': '` / `' — '` welds (`_clean_inline_tabs`), about 59 entries on IZABPD.

The same PR folds these parts back in stage 6 (`stage6.parsing._refold_shattered`, which the lint's own test `_is_shattered` gates). That mends a single-column paragraph: OIEPQD VECUUM 21, re-rendered with it, is one whole paragraph and the lint goes quiet on it. It does not mend any of the 15 above. Every one is a multi-column row printed line by line, so each printed line also carries a neighbouring column's words (the year, title, organisation or role column, and the row's dates), and folding keeps them inside the paragraph. So the lint also fires on a folded paragraph when a line carries a column's dates (`_spliced_column_date`: a date range ending a line that is not the entry's last, or a year opening the entry ahead of a capitalised word). Over fresh renders of the branch head (origin/dev `64bb4541` merged, same 272 runs) it fires on the same 15 entries of the same 3 runs: 14 as folded paragraphs that still carry the column, and RLADNC 540 still as separate paragraphs. All 15 remain open under #1583, for the converter to separate the columns. Ceilings: a spliced role or place column without a date is not seen (every one of the 15 carries a date), and a paragraph whose own heading line ends in its dates, followed by prose that folds, would read as spliced (none seen).

| YUY-SLC | 2026-10-08 | `feat/1588-coverage-contract` on origin/dev `8e29dd24`, against `8e29dd24`, both over one fresh render of `8e29dd24` (`scripts/render_gate.py` with `--source-dir`, 37 of 37 and 63 of 63; `scripts/doctor_gate.py` per farm with `--source-dir`) | 100: YUYVIG 37 (dev-259), **held out**, every source a docx (the 15 uploaded docx, and the 22 PDFs converted locally with the dev-259 converter, `d1e49e39`'s `core/pdf_to_docx.py`, whose element counts match the stored stage-2 `document_length` on all 22); the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm 63, in-sample | `_labels_yuyvig_v2` (held out) and the M1 labels (in-sample); a random 30 unmatched hits of each arm hand-read against the render | #1588 |

YUY-SLC adds `source_line_coverage` (#1588's first fact-accounting step): a source line holding at least 5 pairs of adjacent 3+-letter words, under half of which any one rendered line holds (both words, either order), counting the Appendix and tracked deletions. Not judged: template boilerplate, stage 1a's headings, a line that is a list of names (5d rewrites author names), a protected personal-data value, and every line of a Personal Data (A) entry. One finding per stage-3b entry, INFO. It changes no other lint's findings on any of the 100 runs (base and branch reports identical once its key is dropped).

The thresholds were fitted on the farm only, in three rounds. Pairs of every word read 1,693 findings there (21% matched); dropping digits and one- and two-letter words (5d initials, reformatted dates) 753 (30%); counting a pair rendered when one output line holds both words, in either order, and leaving out name lists, 370 (38%); leaving out Personal Data entries, 359. YUYVIG was scored once, on the final code.

| arm | runs with a hit | findings (lines) | per run: median / p90 / max | matched / located | recall, index match: base to branch | hand-read: true / partial / false |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| YUYVIG, held out | 34 of 37 | 254 (303) | 3 / 15 / 57 | 31 / 248 (13%) | 80 to 96 of 250; HIGH 16 to 21 of 41 | 11 / 9 / 10 of 30 unmatched |
| farm, in-sample | 55 of 63 | 359 (414) | 4 / 13 / 29 | 130 / 352 (37%) | 209 to 251 of 487; HIGH 40 to 43 of 84 | 16 / 7 / 7 of 30 unmatched |

True means the line's content is on no rendered line; partial means the record renders but loses what the line held beyond its title (a role word, a description, a mentee's outcome, a course description, a run of dates); false means the content renders reworded. The false hits are author lists the name-list test misses (a list number or "MD" before the first name: 4 of the 10 held-out), a research-summary paragraph reworded, reworded talk, review and certification rows, and a URL; in-sample also a source table's column-header row, the tail of a citation split across lines, and a DEA registration stage 6 withholds. Most true and partial hits are #1205's supplementary prose (descriptions, duties, award criteria, mentors and advisors), which stage 4 does not extract by design until #1205 lands.

Held out, the matched hits are a lower bound and the unmatched sample is 37% true and 67% true or partial, so, counting every matched hit as true, about 45% of the hits are wholly missing text and about 70% lose something. Under #1625's 50% bar for the run page (and the 80% WARN bar), the lint ships INFO as a review-copy note only (`REVIEW_COPY_ONLY_LINTS`): each quoted source line is an item under "Lines from your CV that CViche couldn't place, often present in another form", at most `MAX_FLAGS_PER_LINT` (25) a document, and the score never reads it. Recall it adds held out, by the YUY-HO definition (a verifier credit or an index match): 140 to 155 of 444, HIGH 20 to 25 of 53 (GFZWQE-01, HNQBOI-01, SPINJC-02, SQMWHM-01, XNWSZN-01). An index match can credit a hit on the right entry for another reason: SQMWHM-01 and XNWSZN-01 are invented values (#1445) on an entry whose other lines the lint names. Per cell, in `doctor/COVERAGE.md`.

Ceilings: a fact inside a line (a number, a date, a grant amount) is not read; a record reworded past half its word pairs reads as lost; a name list with a list number reads as a line; a line found anywhere in the document counts as rendered, even in the wrong section.

| YUY-SLC2 | 2026-10-09 | `feat/1588-slc-precision` on origin/dev `49ed5a4a` (dev-263), against `49ed5a4a`, both over one fresh render of `49ed5a4a` (`scripts/render_gate.py` with `--source-dir`, 37 of 37 and 63 of 63; `scripts/doctor_gate.py` per farm with `--source-dir`) | 100: YUYVIG 37, **held out** (YUY-SLC's sources); the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm 63, in-sample | `_labels_yuyvig_v2` (held out) and the M1 labels (in-sample); a random 30 unmatched hits of each arm, on base and on branch, hand-read against the render (120) | #1588 |

YUY-SLC2 removes the deterministic false-positive classes YUY-SLC named from `source_line_coverage`, each fitted on the farm only:

- A citation's leading author list is left out of the pairs when one rendered line holds a word of at least 3 of its name chunks. A list number, a footnote mark, a trailing year or "and" no longer breaks the list, and a list that 5d cut to six names and "et al." still counts.
- A URL or e-mail address inside a record line is left out, and so is a Word HYPERLINK field code. A line that is little but a link is still judged on the link.
- A tab-separated column-header row is not judged when the next printed line is a tab-separated row holding a digit.
- A DEA registration that carries its number is not judged: stage 6 withholds it (#821).
- A printed line that continues the one before it (it opens in lower case, or closes a bracket or quote it never opened) is judged joined to that line, under the first line's entry.

Not fixed, because no deterministic rule separates them from a loss: reworded research-summary paragraphs, and reworded talk, interview, review, certification and licence rows. The change leaves every other lint's findings unchanged on all 100 runs (base and branch reports identical once its key is dropped).

| arm | runs with a hit | findings (lines) | per run: median / p90 / max | matched / located | recall, index match | hand-read base sample: true / partial / false | hand-read branch sample |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| farm, in-sample | 50 to 49 of 63 | 255 (285) to 227 (252) | 3 / 9 / 22 to 2 / 8 / 19 | 85 / 251 to 74 / 223 | 238 to 233 of 487; HIGH 43 to 43 of 84 | 6 / 10 / 14 | 5 / 10 / 15 |

The rubric is YUY-SLC's. One reader judged both code versions, so base and branch compare; YUY-SLC's 11 / 30 and 16 / 30 do not, being another reader on an older render. Since that render, #1205's sub-points print much of the supplementary prose YUY-SLC counted true, as tracked deletions the lint counts as rendered.

Every true and partial hit of both base samples still fires on the branch (24 of 24). Of their false positives, 7 of 22 held out and 3 of 14 in-sample no longer fire, which on those samples moves precision from 4 / 30 to 4 / 23 held out and from 6 / 30 to 6 / 27 in-sample.

The branch samples' 30 false positives:
- Held out (15): reworded rows (10: talks, interviews and media mentions, reviews, a certification, a position, a hospital); a research-summary paragraph (FLBFRK 7); an author list written "Surname, Given" (XNWSZN 550, 586); a column-header row split over two printed lines (IZABPD 420); and a line that is only a redirect link (ECXGAT 1747).
- In-sample (15): reworded rows (14), and a column-header row whose next line holds no digit (YYVHNN 472).

Neither of the 50% and 80% bars is met: strict precision is 4 / 30 (13%) held out and 5 / 30 (17%) in-sample, and 15 / 30 in both arms counting partials. So the lint stays INFO and a review-copy note only (`REVIEW_COPY_ONLY_LINTS`). Recall lost in-sample, all index matches:
- BZZNRL-01 and BZZNRL-02 were matched through entry 7's e-mail line, which now reads too short once its HYPERLINK field code is dropped.
- NDXXAD-06, YOXXOH-uid-1 and YYVHNN-04 were matched through a wrapped line that is now judged with the line before it, and is either half covered or reported under that line's entry.

No held-out finding is lost: IZABPD-02, the one it stops matching, is caught by another lint. The mutation harness's rates are unchanged: `MUTATIONS.md` regenerates identical, and `drop_publication` and `drop_grant` are still caught by this lint.

Ceilings, beyond YUY-SLC's:
- An author list written "Surname, Given" puts each surname in a one-word chunk, which the name test does not count.
- A column-header row is seen only when its cells are tab-separated and the next line is a data row with a digit.
- A continuation joined to a rendered line can be covered past half while it alone was lost (a description, a venue).

Disclosure: a first held-out branch sample was read on an earlier version, whose author-list guard needed a word of every chunk. Two of its false positives (IXJMKS 752, ECXGAT 658) had the shape that the in-sample HFAJCC 173 and PFBSNH 102 also show: 5d's "et al." cut, and a two-letter surname. The guard was relaxed to 3 chunks on that in-sample evidence. The held-out figures above are a fresh sample of the final code.

| SLC-A3 | 2026-10-16 | `feat/1588-slc-alignment` at `b551c0f9` (merged with origin/dev `7879b12b`), against a `git archive` of `7879b12b`: `run_doctor` per run, both arms over the same stored delivered artifacts (each run's own docx, its source docx, stage JSON and prompt logs; nothing rendered, no LLM) | 197 stored runs of the labelled batches, EMHFMB held out: EBYSBC 40, s7ab 10, pilot 12, NDMRSO 29, NECRGC 34, JAFPFP 6, YUYVIG 37, OIEPQD 14, EOAHMI 9, X6 6. The lint runs on the 167 with a source docx | the ground-truth R1 labels (outside the repo: ids, severities and entry indices, no CV text; 1,683 with an entry index; `scripts/doctor_vs_autopsy.py` index match, per batch, each arm with its own script); a fresh blind sample of 160 hits read by four readers; every branch hit on the six EMHFMB runs, read by the verifier (no longer clean, below) | #1588 |

SLC-A3 replaces `source_line_coverage`'s word-pair test. The lint judges the same lines as before (`_rendered_share` is not None), so no line falls under the six-word gate, but it decides each one by aligning it one-to-one to its own place in the delivered docx, read in the accepted view with tracked insertions in. That place is one rendered line, a table's rows, two paragraphs, or, for a line that lists several periods (several talks, or several years of one award), one row per period. A line is reported when its place holds under half of its facts and words, each weighted by its rarity among the CV's printed lines, or misses a year the line states. Each rendered token serves one record only, so a lost near-duplicate of a rendered record is reported. Beyond YUY-SLC2's exclusions, headings and lead-ins are not judged. A research-activities (M1) line may be found in the stage-4.5 summary, a record the CV lists twice shares one rendering, and a rendered citation's pages hold the years a source citation gives as pages. `_rendered_share`, `_Output` and `_printed_lines` are unchanged, so `stage2_text_break` is too.

It was built in three rounds on these runs, each judged blind (#1588). Round 3 changed two things. Among spans of several units the share now ranks before the unit count, so a line goes to its own record's rows, not to fewer rows of a neighbour's table. And a line listing several periods may be found in one row per period: each row must state its period, hold one of the line's above-average-weight words, and be at least two thirds this line's own weight (`ALIGN_GROUP_MIN_OWN`), at most 6 periods (`ALIGN_GROUP_MAX`; raising it to 12 changes no hit here).

Recall by any lint: 870 to 923 of 1,683. 23 labels base matched are no longer matched (4 high), and 18 of those are still caught by another lint. The 5 that leave the doctor are GOWQKA-N2 (a misplaced record that renders in full, which the word pairs caught by accident), RINASX-11, VPMMFM-09, YOXXOH-uid-2 and QZWBKQ-08. 142 are newly matched (47 high). Against round 2's branch it loses one label, CTWLTR-04 (low): a record stage 6 printed under another record's year, which a token alignment cannot see. Every other lint's findings, `stage2_text_break`'s among them (10 in each arm, on the four runs it was built for too), are identical on 197 of 197 runs.

Injection (the slc2 copy of the ground-truth `inject.py`, LLM off, its 10 runs, 2 targets per class, both arms run fresh): of the 58 manifested whole-record drops (`drop_record_s6`, `_s3b` and `_s2`), the lint catches 44 on the right entry (base 19) and 50 anywhere (base 20), and misses none that base catches. Round 3's two changes move the SLC findings of none of the 526 variants from round 2's. Every other lint's digest is identical on every variant and control, and the controls are deterministic on 10 of 10.

Precision, from a fresh blind sample drawn after this measurement (seed "r3": 60 shared, 60 branch-only and 40 base-only hits over 118 runs, at most 3 per run; four readers, none told a hit's stratum; a month dropped where the template prints only the year counts as reworded). Shared hits: 26 wholly lost, 27 partly, 5 reworded, 2 not content. Branch-only: 12, 37, 11, 0. Base-only: 3, 18, 19, 0. Weighted by stratum size, the branch reads 0.846 counting partial losses (standard error 0.033) and 0.303 strict, against the word-pair rule's 0.800 and 0.350 on the same draw; re-weighted by each run's share of its stratum, 0.830. Round 2, before round 3's two changes, read 0.786 (strict 0.211) against 0.736 (0.273).

The six EMHFMB runs were held out of every build round, but round 2's verify read them once and found there the two defects round 3 fixed, so they are no longer a clean held-out set. Read again after round 3, every branch hit on them: 7 wholly lost, 16 partly, 10 reworded (23 / 33 = 0.697 with partials, Wilson 0.53 to 0.83; strict 0.212), against the word-pair rule's 7 / 17 (0.412). Round 2's figure there, 25 / 35, reads 23 / 35 on the same rule: two of its partials render every fact. The bar set before round 3 was 0.80 blind and 0.70 on those six runs; it met the first and missed the second by one hit, and ships on the owner's decision (#1588, 2026-10-19). Severity stays INFO and review-copy only (`REVIEW_COPY_ONLY_LINTS`), at most `MAX_FLAGS_PER_LINT` notes per document.

YUY-DDR measures what #666's residual dedup refusals do to the doctor. The doctor code is the same in every arm, so every change comes from the render. After its review round the branch refuses 30 of dev's 418 dedup decisions and makes 1 new one (389). Counting the farm and stored copies of the 4 shared runs once, that is 21 decisions: H 6, Q4D 6, Q3 3, P 2, I 2, K1 1 and Q4B 1, and none on T, an S code, M, C or a fragment. 20 entries now render, one row each (29 rows on 13 runs with the copies, none removed, none the same as another row of its document). The 21st, TXTATQ 895 (I), is dropped against another kept entry, and its document is unchanged. YUYVIG goes from 1,079 findings to 1,075, the farm from 2,632 to 2,630, the 3 other runs stay at 228, and the stored runs go from 841 to 836. Every changed finding is `dedup_drops` or `source_line_coverage`, and none is new. `dedup_drops` WARN goes silent on CYOFWJ (entry 40, its one drop; farm and stored), and lists fewer drops on IZABPD (125 gone, 3 to 2), LTTYWI (297, 307, 311 and 351 gone, 7 to 3), VGHNZD (275 gone, 2 to 1) and stored LFFKVV (1572 gone, 7 to 6). `source_line_coverage` INFO goes silent on LTTYWI 297, 307, 311 and 351 (YUYVIG and stored), each an entry that now renders, and on VGHNZD 286 (farm), which is dropped in both arms: the newly rendered 275 row moves the lint's own-place match for 286's line, so a still-dropped entry loses its one finding. That is the only finding the branch silences on an entry it does not render. Read by hand, the 32 decision rows `2f4b56ea` changed (copies counted) are 28 distinct records kept, 1 correct new drop (TXTATQ 895, against 721), 2 refusals that changed nothing because the entry was dropped against another row (TXTATQ 895 and LFFKVV 1487), and 1 wrong new drop: LFFKVV 1487 (Q4D) against 1488, another journal. The review round removes that one, and 1487 renders once. So each of the 6 `dedup_drops` WARN drops that now render (CYOFWJ 40, IZABPD 125, LTTYWI 297, 307, 311 and 351) was a distinct record lost; the 2026-10-08 history row read IZABPD 125 and LTTYWI 297, 307, 311 and 351 the same way. The per-lint row (M1-dup) is not re-measured here. Recall: YUYVIG goes from 96 to 95 of 250 labelled findings (HIGH 19 to 18 of 41). The one lost is IZABPD-06 (HIGH, #666, entry 125), which on dev only `dedup_drops` matched: its record now renders, so no drop is left to flag. There `dedup_drops` keeps its 10 hits and goes from 2 matched to 1, and `source_line_coverage` goes from 117 to 113 hits, with 14 matched and 9 caught in both arms. BLBVPD-03 (medium, entry 213) and IXJMKS-V1 (low, entry 866), #666 losses no lint matched on dev, now render, which recall cannot count. The farm stays at 252 of 487 (HIGH 49 of 84): `dedup_drops` goes from 14 hits to 13 and `source_line_coverage` from 349 to 348, with the same matched (3 and 110) and caught (3 and 84) in both arms. The second render of `33d0d31c` matches the first: 418 decisions in both, `scripts/render_gate_compare.py` 0 paragraph and 0 fingerprint changes on all 3 farms, and every doctor finding and the recall the same. Against `2f4b56ea`, the review round changes one decision (stored LFFKVV 1487 is no longer dropped against 1488) and one row (LFFKVV, +1). The farms' documents, every doctor total and the recall are the same, and the stored LFFKVV `dedup_drops` WARN lists 6 drops instead of 7 (on `2f4b56ea`, 1487 had taken 1572's place). Its two other narrowings (a society's or an award's name with its words reordered is one name; a class year that is the kept text's only year dates the award) change no decision on these runs, so only synthetic tests cover them. Not seen: the blind review's 126-run farm, of which 2 runs were checked (2071_Z, a distinct I society kept on both branch commits; web174, a K1 drop paired with another kept row, the same on both); a panel listed twice in one year, once as ad hoc, which now prints twice (synthetic); and an unquoted book title opening with a bare journal name, which still vouches for that journal's drop in every arm (synthetic). The figures are `79691487`'s doctor, which adds #2018 (`source_line_coverage` reads only a line's own entry's deletions), over the renders of `79691487` and `803baf7c`. Those renders' dedup decisions and sidecars are the same as `33d0d31c`'s and `8717210e`'s on all 125 runs, and their documents differ only in the render date. #2018 moves the same findings in both arms, all `source_line_coverage` INFO and none on an entry this branch changes: 4 appear on the farm (YUY-SLCO's BMAMWE 1320 and 1368, BMHBJZ 389 and VGHNZD 614), and 13 change only their lowest share (YUY-SLCO's 12, and stored JFBPNC 674). So every difference between the arms is the same as with `33d0d31c`'s doctor, which gave the farm 2,628 and 2,626 findings and 345 and 344 `source_line_coverage` hits. Every other figure here is the same with either doctor.

| YUY-SPO | 2026-10-19 | `feat/1205-n3-subpoints`'s doctor commit (a sub-point vouches only for its own entry), against its parent `5379b87b`, both over one fresh render of that commit with sub-points on (`scripts/render_gate.py --source-dir`; `scripts/doctor_gate.py` per farm, `--source-dir`) | 102, as YUY-SPN | as YUY-SPN | #1205 |

YUY-SPO measures the doctor fix for YUY-SPN's false silence. Stage 6 now records in the render-warnings sidecar (`subpoint_placements`) the entry each sub-point was written for and the code that entry rendered as. Given an entry's index, `offered_as_subpoint` matches only that entry's own sub-points; without the record (an older sidecar, or none) it matches every sub-point in the document, as before. The render is the same in both arms apart from the new sidecar key: against a render of `5379b87b`, every run's `w:delText`, sidecar warnings and dedup decisions are identical, and its `w:t` differ only in the render date. Over it, 1 finding of 102 runs changes: `unrendered_records` WARN on VGHNZD 477 (N3A) returns, with the message and evidence it had in YUY-SPN's dev arm. Before #1936, the farm goes from 2,353 findings to 2,354, YUYVIG stays at 975 and the 3 other runs at 78. The 422 placements of the 102 runs each name an entry stage 4 holds, no two stage-4 entries share a placement's index, and each of the 492 sub-point lines in the documents belongs to exactly one placement. The returned WARN matches no verified finding (`unrendered_records` on the farm: 5 hits, 4 matched, 3 caught), so recall does not move: 236 of 487 on the farm (HIGH 46 of 84) and 95 of 250 on YUYVIG (HIGH 21 of 41). A follow-up gives a tab-joined child appointment (`_child_position_records`) its parent's index, so the duties split off a child's title are that entry's own sub-point; before it they were placed under no entry, and a synthetic case raised a false `unrendered_records` WARN. No farm child has duties: re-rendered with it, all 102 sidecars and every document part (revision dates masked) are unchanged, and so is every finding.

The 3 `shattered_prose` findings fired on dev's content overflow: a low-coverage L3 role (coverage under 50%, text over 300 characters) has its whole source text written in the section in place of its line, and the lint read that text. When the sub-point and the role's line hold every part of that text, the overflow is skipped, so the role keeps its line and its prose is a tracked deletion. 4 of the 10 entries (DYLJXC 868, RLADNC 563, SQMWHM 1482, WIANVH 689) have no finding in either arm. Read by hand, 10 of the 11 sub-points are the role's own prose (what the role oversaw, built or achieved), and 1 is junk: RLADNC 563, the role's title again and then a clause cut off before its object. The sub-points cost no recovered line (#1205 comment 6275741663): the #221 recovery re-emits the same lines in both arms with sub-points on and off, none of them L3. With sub-points off, every document and sidecar is the same in both arms. With them on, only the 7 runs with an L3 sub-point change their fingerprint, and 4 of them their paragraphs (`w:t`), all in the Clinical Leadership span: the overflow skip on IZABPD 477 and 488, RLADNC 567, SQMWHM 1482 and WIANVH 689. Accept All gives dev's document on the other 98 runs. On those 4 it lacks 7 lines that dev's overflow prints: 6 are in Reject All as the sub-point, and 1, SQMWHM 1482's source line, is in neither document as written, though the role's line prints every word of it. YUY-SLCO measures the doctor fix for #1205 comment 6388379186 item 1. `source_line_coverage` read any entry's tracked deletion as a place a source line could be found, so another entry's struck text that shared a line's facts silenced it (BMAMWE 1368 in YUY-SPPU). Each deleted line now has owners. A #1205 sub-point (author `SUBPOINT_AUTHOR`) belongs to the entries whose placements in the render sidecar (`subpoint_placements`) wrote every paragraph of it, and a spanning sub-point row's joined line counts as the row. With no placements recorded, a sub-point belongs to every line, as `offered_as_subpoint` reads it. Any other deletion (on the two renders, always the enrichment's w:del of a citation's CV line: 3,011 lines, 3,009 held by one entry's text and 2 by two) belongs to the stage-3b entries whose text holds it; one that no entry's text holds belongs to every line. A line reads a deletion when one of the stage-3b entries holding the line owns it, or when no entry holds the line. `stage2_text_break` reads the accepted view only and is unchanged in every arm. The rule reaches the deletions that are not sub-points because the defect is theirs too: BMAMWE 1368's cover on dev is another citation's (1271) enrichment w:del, freed when 1348's S1 sub-point took the claims that had held it, and a variant that reads only sub-points per entry leaves 1368 silent (blind review). So runs with sub-points off, and stored runs written before placements existed, move as well.

Every figure above was measured on `0a22ed16` and again on its review round, `7469256d`: the findings are the same on every run of every arm. The review round reads a sub-point paragraph that itself holds the cell joiner (" | ") whole. Before it, a spanning row with such a paragraph was split mid-paragraph, read as no sub-point's, and vouched for every line again. None of the farms' 650 placed paragraphs holds the joiner, so no finding moves. Two limits are written into the code, and neither has a case on the farms. First, a sub-point's owners are the sidecar's indexes and a line's own entries are stage 3b's, and the two are not reconciled. A placement under an index that no stage-3b entry holding the line carries (another index, or none), or a sub-point whose written text differs from its placement's paragraph, vouches for none of that line. Every one of the 563 placements names a stage-3b index and every sub-point line is a placement's; 1 of the 650 paragraphs (YUYVIG DYLJXC 661, D3) is not in its own entry's stage-3b text, and the sidecar attributes it. Second, a record the CV lists twice, in two entries, shares one rendering in the accepted view (SLC-A3's twin rule) but not a tracked deletion: that is the owner's per-entry rule taken literally. None of the 4 findings that appear with sub-points on is such a twin (blind review). Not addressed: #1205 comment 6368763566 item 6. `offered_as_subpoint` still counts a record line as delivered when its own entry's sub-point repeats its words, and the lint keeps reading a line's own entry's sub-point, as the owner's rule requires.

YUY-SPRS measures the restated-prefix strip. A fragment splits only on tabs, line breaks and pipes, so when the prose shares its entry's line the whole line was the sub-point, and Reject All printed the row's content twice. The strip cuts one contiguous span of words the line prints, opening the fragment or after a word that closes a part of it, holding the line's first two distinctive words, closing where a part closes, not running on past the cut, and leaving words that start cleanly (the clean-start round, below). On `e439d2c9` the cut changes 119 sub-points on 41 runs (D1 3, R 8, N3A 3, N3B 14, Q2 20, B1 2, Q4B 3, Q4C 5, L3 3, K1 24, I 1, H 19, C 1, P 9, S1 2, S2 2); `12bd1c9c` changed 132 on 42. One, VVRTUC 802 (K1), only restated its line and writes nothing. The words check holds on all 119 (and held on `12bd1c9c`'s 132): every word cut is one the line prints, read on the planner's line and on Reject All's row, no word is added, and over every run the words Reject All loses are exactly the changed entries' cut words (824). Accept All gives the same document as dev on all 102 runs, and with sub-points off the document, its comments and the sidecar are the same on all 102 (`scripts/render_gate_compare.py` passes on the three farms). Counting the row and each of its sub-point paragraphs holding half or more of the row's distinctive words, Reject All prints the row's content once where it printed it twice for 64 of the 119 (69 of 132 on `12bd1c9c`); 35 still hold half or more after the cut (a later part of the line, repeated; 40 on `12bd1c9c`), and 20 held under half before it (23). The #221 recovery re-emits the same lines in both arms, sub-points on and off, and no line ends in neither document that does not in dev. The synthetic probe's 195 cases change 16 sub-points, with no words-check failure and Accept All equal to sub-points off in every case.

The doctor reads a cut sub-point as the line the CV wrote. On `70a2897d` it read only the cut paragraph, and `source_line_coverage`, which aligns a source line to one output unit, reported 25 lines split between their row and their sub-point (606 to 631 by the review's count; 613 to 638 counting the findings that ran here): FCAAUV 7, ECXGAT 1479 and ULHCAP 1415 on YUYVIG; BMHBJZ 178, EQGGRB 125, KDAZOM 417, 420, 448 and 458, MUHLLD 205, 207, 212 and 220, QITQWH 47 and 50, QSWXKR 100 and 109, and VGHNZD 79, 80, 84, 86, 89, 94, 582 and 647 on the farm, among them entries earlier family rows recorded going silent when their sub-points landed (FCAAUV 7 in YUY-SPB, ECXGAT 1479 in YUY-SPN, KDAZOM 417 in YUY-SPIA, EQGGRB 125 in YUY-SPPU). Under dev `79691487`'s doctor `70a2897d`'s render reports the same 25. The review round records, per placement, the line each cut paragraph was written from (`SubpointPlacement.lines`); the sub-point view reads that line with the paragraph while the document holds the paragraph (`subpoint_text`), and `source_line_coverage` reads the tracked deletions with each cut paragraph as its line (`as_written_deletions`); `coverage.py` is unchanged. Each arm's own doctor: findings go from 3,935 on `33d0d31c` to 3,939 on `12bd1c9c`, and every difference is YUY-SLCO's (4 `source_line_coverage` findings appear and 12 change only their share, none on an entry with a changed sub-point), but one: VVRTUC's `duplicate_records` WARN, whose evidence counts one copy fewer, the K1 802 sub-point that is now written nowhere. Against dev `79691487`'s doctor over dev's render, `12bd1c9c` is the same on every run but that one. `unrendered_records` (6), `under_extraction` (0), `offschema_fields` (62) and `grant_facts` (44) are the same in every arm; through `run_stage6`, a two-line entry whose record line restated its row was reported absent by `unrendered_records` on `70a2897d` and is not on `12bd1c9c`. Recall: YUYVIG stays at 96 of 250 (HIGH 19 of 41) and the farm at 252 of 487 (HIGH 49 of 84), as on dev. `70a2897d` had YUYVIG at 98: FCAAUV-05 (low, #817) and ULHCAP-01 (high, #1251), each a different defect matched by index by one of the 25 findings. After the clean-start round and dev `74002b7f`, each arm's own doctor reports 3,932 findings in both arms (YUYVIG 1,074, the farm 2,630, extra 228), the same on every run but that VVRTUC WARN, and recall is the same in both: YUYVIG 94 of 250 (HIGH 18 of 41) and the farm 252 of 487 (HIGH 49 of 84), `source_line_coverage` 113 hits (14 matched, 9 caught) and 348 (110, 84). Dev's own changes since `79691487` (#2021 to #2023) take YUYVIG from 96 to 94 in both arms: IZABPD-06 (by `dedup_drops`) and VTRKUF-15 (by `date_cell_shape`) are no longer caught, neither on an entry with a changed sub-point.

The review round's other changes. A cut takes only the separator words at its edges, so a mark joined to a letter or a digit stays as the CV wrote it (on invented text `70a2897d` wrote `.5` as `5`, `-40%` as `40%` and `/s/` as `s/`); and a span may not take one half of a bracket or quote pair the line matched. Of `70a2897d`'s 137 changed sub-points, 129 are written the same, 5 are back as the CV wrote them (ECXGAT 1441 and HFAJCC 542, 543, 588 and 590, mentees whose span ended inside their parenthesis), ECXGAT 1477 and 1479 now lose only the mentee's name, with the parenthesis whole, and BMHBJZ 462 keeps a dash joined to a word. Over 30,000 invented fragments, `12bd1c9c` cuts 6,223, each one block of whole words, none a word its line does not print, and none leaving a bracket unmatched that the fragment matched; `70a2897d` cut 458 into a word and left 192 brackets unmatched. Still as the CV wrote it, by design: a restatement closing the fragment, running on past the cut, taking half a bracket, or not opening with the line's first distinctive words (158 unchanged sub-points still hold half or more of their row's distinctive words). A blind hand read of `70a2897d`'s 137 changed sub-points found 96 clean strips, 22 that still restate a later part of the row (the venue, place and year, a course description, a mentee's current position, an awarding body, a citation's journal and pages), 14 broken in their text, 4 stripped of words the rest needs to read, and 1 unclear (RLADNC 563, cut short upstream by interleaved columns). The review round writes 5 of the 14 broken ones as the CV wrote them (ECXGAT 1441 and HFAJCC 542, 543, 588 and 590) and re-cuts ECXGAT 1477 and 1479 (broken) and BMHBJZ 462 (clean); those 3 were not read again. The 7 broken that remain (IZIXVF 347 and 365, BMHBJZ 400, EQGGRB 111, MRJDWE 104, VNUAHA 251 and 265) and the 4 stripped too far (ECXGAT 2129, BMHBJZ 507, JNATFN 118, VNUAHA 305) lose no word: each is the CV's text less one span the row prints. Their shapes are a cut inside a sentence, a date or page range of the row (2 of them in a short form) left ahead of the new words, a label, a preposition or an author name left before the cut, and a sentence whose subject was the cut span.

The clean-start round (`6a778c2e`) writes such a line as the CV wrote it, the approved fallback, when the words a cut would leave do not start cleanly (`_starts_cleanly`). Each exit is keyed on the text: the words after the cut open with three words the row prints, with a bracket closed by a colon, with a range of the row's numbers in full or in a short form, with a function word the cut runs straight into, or with a relative pronoun or an appositive; or a cut inside the line follows a lower-case word ending in a colon, or a comma, the cut closing the sentence it began ahead of a new one. All 11 broken or over-stripped rows above are back as the CV wrote them, and so are ECXGAT 1477 and 1479. None of the 96 clean strips, none of the 22 that still restate a later part and not RLADNC 563 changes; each exit was narrowed until it flipped none of the 96. Against `12bd1c9c`'s render, those 13 sub-points are the only ones whose text changes (no row's text moves with dev's #2021, #2022 or #2023); read by hand, each now restates its row in full, as the CV wrote it, with no broken text. Five invented recovery tests that pinned a cut after a role or a mentee's period now pin the line as written. The lost-line table holds the same 11 lines in both arms, 3 in neither document in both (SQMWHM), and 2 lines held by row plus sub-point (IZABPD); the Reject All doubles are 2 on dev and 6 on the branch, as on `12bd1c9c`.

| GF-1 | 2026-10-09 | `feat/1588-grant-fact-accounting` on origin/dev `49ed5a4a`, against `49ed5a4a`, both over one fresh render of `49ed5a4a` (`scripts/render_gate.py` with `--source-dir`, 39 of 39 and 63 of 63; `scripts/doctor_gate.py` per farm with `--source-dir`) | 102: YUYVIG 39 (37 labelled, dev-259), **held out**, every source a docx (the 17 uploaded docx, and the 22 PDFs converted locally with `d1e49e39`'s `core/pdf_to_docx.py`); the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot farm 63, in-sample | `_labels_yuyvig_v2` (held out) and the M1 labels (in-sample); every hit hand-read against the entry's stage-4 record, its source text and its rendered grant table | #1588, #1575 |

GF-1 adds `grant_facts`, #1588's first fact-accounting slice: the grant numbers and dollar amounts of each M2A-M2C entry, source against render. `value_not_in_source` is a number or amount on the entry's rendered grant table (its Award Source, title and costs rows, which stage 4's fields fill) that the entry's text does not state in any format; the message names the other grant entries whose text does, which is #1575's copy. `value_missing` is a number or amount the entry's text states (an amount needs its "$" or "USD") that is neither on its table nor anywhere outside the other grants' tables: the Appendix, a note, a tracked-deleted sub-point. Values match through reformatting: an amount by its dollars within half the last digit it states ("$1.5M" is $1,500,000; "$12, 345" and "$123.000" as PDFs write them), an NIH number by institute and serial ("5R01 CA012345-01A1" is "CA-12345"), and the DoD, ONR, NSF, VA HSR&D, AHA and ClinicalTrials.gov formats seen on both farms by their letters and digits. The tables are read with `w:ins` text and without `w:delText`; the sub-points are the `w:delText` stage 6 offers them as. A table is matched to the entry it was rendered from by its title and then by the amounts, number and years it shows. One finding per entry and shape, INFO. It changes no other lint's findings on any of the 102 runs (base and branch reports identical once its key is dropped), and adds 25 findings on 4 YUYVIG runs and 27 on 5 farm runs.

Fitted on the farm only. Matching tables to entries by title, as `role_consistency` does, read 101 findings there, 40 of them `value_not_in_source`: nearly all were renewals sharing a title, which stage 6 renders newest first and a title match pairs in stage-4 order, so each read as carrying the other's amount. Matching by the values each table shows took it to 35, and reading spaced and dotted thousands to 27. YUYVIG was then scored once: 27 findings on 5 runs, 25 true and 2 false. The false ones are XNWSZN 1192, a grant number a PDF typed with "l" for "1" that stage 4 corrected, and SIJYJZ 1065, a role word before a bare NSF number read as its directorate. Both were fixed after scoring (an "l" or "O" beside a digit reads as the digit; an NSF directorate has 3 or 4 letters), which removes those 2 findings on YUYVIG and changes nothing on the farm, so those two rules are in-sample.

| arm | runs with a hit | `value_missing` | `value_not_in_source` | matched / hits | recall, index match: base to branch | true / judged |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| YUYVIG, held out, as scored | 5 of 39 | 25 | 2 | 2 / 27 | 92 to 92 of 250; HIGH 21 to 21 of 41 | 25 / 27: `value_missing` 24 / 25, `value_not_in_source` 1 / 2 |
| farm, in-sample | 5 of 63 | 26 | 1 | 9 / 27 | 238 to 240 of 487; HIGH 43 to 44 of 84 | 27 / 27 |

True means the value is that grant's and the document does not show it (`value_missing`), or the table shows a value its own entry does not state (`value_not_in_source`). Of the 50 true `value_missing` hits, 46 are a second amount of the same grant beside the one that renders: an annual or total direct cost, an indirect cost or a yearly amount, where stage 4 keeps one amount and the template's "Annual direct costs:" row renders empty (RTBEJA's 13 entries state "Annual direct costs:" in those words). The other 4 are a second grant of a multi-grant entry (NJIKGI 338, YYVHNN 481), a combined total over an entry's three records (SIJYJZ 1210), and #1575's own lost amount (UVZNIC 592). Both true `value_not_in_source` hits are copies: UVZNIC 592 shows entry 603's amount (UVZNIC-01, HIGH), and QZWBKQ 899 renders entry 900's grant whole (QZWBKQ-05). On YUYVIG the lint adds no index recall: `year_not_in_source` already names UVZNIC 592, and the 23 other hits sit on entries no label names. On the farm it adds YYVHNN-01 (HIGH, E5) and YYVHNN-05 (E14).

Severity: `value_missing` is 50 of 51 true (held out 24 of 25 as scored), over the 80% WARN bar, but 46 of those are one shape on 5 CVs, and a run-page row needs approved wording; `value_not_in_source` is 2 of 3, on 3 findings. Both ship INFO as a review-copy comment on the grant's title (`REVIEW_COPY_ONLY_LINTS`), and the score never reads them (INFO weighs 0). Ceilings: an amount in another currency, a number in a format not listed, a grant fact outside M2A-M2C (a trial), and dates, effort and roles are not read; a value found anywhere outside the other grants' tables counts as shown, even in the wrong section.

CEV-1 adds `cross_entry_value`: a stage-4 value that its own entry's text lacks and another entry of the same stage-4 call states. Stage 4 sends each batch of `STAGE4_BATCH_SIZE` (10) entries to the model in one call per taxonomy code; the lint rebuilds those calls from stage 3b's entry order and checks each value with `stage4/grounding.py`'s token checks (years, amounts, efforts, grant numbers; own text read generously, the other entry's strictly), plus two kinds grounding leaves alone: `pi_role` and a person's name (`pi_name`, `mentee_name`, `co_investigators` and the like). The call rebuild was checked against the stored stage-4 prompt logs of the 39 YUYVIG runs: 3,338 of 3,343 multi-entry group prompts are exactly one rebuilt group, and the 5 others are smaller prompts inside one (the recovery pass). Rebuilt from stage 4's own order instead, 416 of them straddle two groups, because stage 4 sorts a table's string index last.

Re-measured under #1840 (`fix/1840-grounding-fte-and-mistyped-year` on origin/dev `7e41cd29`), after `stage4/grounding.py`'s own-text reading began to accept an effort written as an FTE fraction and a year mistyped by one keystroke in a date shape: 24 findings on 19 files (18 uids). Read by hand: 13 true, 5 partial, 6 false, so 13 / 24 (54%). The first measurement, with origin/dev's reading, was 28 findings on 22 files (21 uids), 13 / 28 (46%); the 4 that went are the four false hits in that reading's own shapes (below), and none was added. 54% clears the 50% a WARN needs, but in-sample: every rule was written from these runs, and the four removed were removed because they were known to be false. The lint stays INFO until a held-out set is read.

Held-out read, 2026-10-16 (origin/dev `99a11df6`, lint unchanged): every S3 run whose uid is not among the 392 above, 224 runs dated 2026-05-26 to 2026-10-14, each with stage 3b, none sharing a sha256 with the 400. As a control, the same code on the 400 still gives 24 findings on 18 uids. On the held-out runs: 18 findings on 12 uids, read by hand with the rules above: 4 true, 4 partial, 10 false, so **4 / 18 (22%)**. 8 / 18 (44%) with partials counted true. Pooled with the in-sample read: 17 / 42 (40%). The WARN bar is not met, and the lint stays INFO. The false hits fall into three kinds, none of them seen in-sample:
- 7 are one record that stage 2 cut in two, so the "other entry" is the same record's other half (ASRNQM, OWSAZN, PZQJKL, ZGNARO, WLUZVM 842/864). Four of these come from tables that put one field or line per row.
- 3 are a `pi_role` the lint missed because the words were reordered ("(Yale) Principal Investigator", RUPGMT 262/269/277).
- 1 is a total that stage 4 correctly computed as the annual amount times the years (MQZMMA 171).

The 4 partials are "Fall/Spring YYYY" rows whose source does not say which year the term ends (EKGTXD, PESCOB 205/206; the two look like one CV run twice). Any lint change written from these 18 makes them in-sample, so a promotion would need another held-out read after it.

- True (13): UVZNIC 592 (603's dates, amount and effort, 594's and 603's "PI" for its "MPI") and 559 (573's mentee as a second record), VNUAHA 262 (end 2004 for its 2003-2006), VVRTUC 43 and 45 (a 1973 start for "July 2010 -"), web199 1120 and 1157 (5% for a stated 2.5%), 2054 66.16 (2011-2014 for 2010-2013), web205 139 (2015 for 2014), JNATFN 380 (2021 for 2022-2025, in two stored copies), web056 874 and web062 1022 (the owner, a stated PI, given the co-investigator or co-PI role the next grant states).
- Partial (5): the value is not in the entry's text and another entry does state it, but the right value cannot be read off the source: ECXGAT 832 ("Feb. 21 - 24" with no event year), GJXIWD 168 (an end year cut to "202"), YYVHNN 39 (a garbled table row), web205 849 (a grant naming another PI and no role for the owner, given "Co-PI"), 976WPY 122 (a narrative paragraph dated from the next one).
- False (6): five are one record that stage 2 cut in two, so the "other entry" is the same grant or mentee (QZWBKQ 899 and 900, web246 355, YIXQFB 2222, UJNBIT 296); `grant_boundary` and `record_boundary` are the lints for that cut. One is an entry whose text is "As above" (web065 65).
- No longer reported (4, #1840): a correct value that the source writes in a shape grounding's own-text reading did not accept, an effort as a fraction (RNKYST 197 and 206, "0.100 FTE" for 10%) and a mistyped year (web172 123.0 "2009-20011", 2068 591 "3/31/3019"). Stage 4 nulled and re-extracted these same values; #1840 widened that shared reading, and these four were false hits on the first measurement.
- Not caught: the lint judges a year only on an entry whose text states some year, and a role only on a grant whose text labels some role. A year or role the model supplies for an entry that states none is supplied, not borrowed; `year_not_in_source` reports the year. The first draft, without those two rules and with a looser role match, had 31 hits on the 39 YUYVIG runs, 2 of them borrowed values (UVZNIC 559 and 592).
- What it adds over `year_not_in_source`: 10 of the 24 hits are on entries `year_not_in_source` does not flag, 4 of them true (web199 1120 and 1157, web056 874, web062 1022); 12 of 28 before #1840, the other two being RNKYST 197 and 206. `year_not_in_source` reads years with its own predicate, not grounding's, and still reports web172 123.0 and 2068 591.

Matched / hits on labelled runs (YUYVIG v2 and the EBYSBC/s7ab/pilot labels, 99 runs): 5 of 12 (5 of 14 before #1840; the two RNKYST hits it removed were on an EBYSBC run and matched no label), catching UVZNIC-01, UVZNIC-09, VVRTUC-01 and QZWBKQ-05.
